     ```python
     def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
         state: StateModel,
         patterns: list[str],
         *,
         plan: PlanModel | None = None,
         status_filter: str = "open",
     ) -> list[str]:
         # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
         plan_ids: set[str] = set()
         if plan is not None:
             plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
             plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
             for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
                 plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
         for pattern in patterns:
             matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
             if matches:
                 for finding in matches:
                     fid = finding["id"]
                     if fid not in seen:
                         seen.add(fid)
                         result.append(fid)
             elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
                 seen.add(pattern)
                 result.append(pattern)
         return result
     ```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→
   147→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   148→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   149→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   150→    referenced_ids = {
   151→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   152→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   153→    }
   154→
   155→    # Check each referenced ID
   156→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   157→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   158→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   159→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   160→                result.changes += 1
   161→
   162→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   163→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   164→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   165→
   166→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   167→    if resurfaced:
   168→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   169→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   170→
   171→    # Prune old superseded entries
   172→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   173→    result.pruned = pruned
   174→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   175→
   176→    return result
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "ReconcileResult",
   181→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   182→]
   183→
```

```
1→"""Sync stale subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Handles both directions:
     4→  1. **Cleanup** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale.
     5→  2. **Inject**  — when the queue is empty after cleanup, add stale dimensions.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→
    15→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    20→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    21→
    22→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→
    25→    @property
    26→    def changes(self) -> int:
    27→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    32→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    33→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    34→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    38→    if not dim_scores:
    39→        return set()
    40→
    41→    stale: set[str] = set()
    42→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    43→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    44→            continue
    45→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    46→        if dim_key:
    47→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    48→    return stale
    49→
    50→
    51→def sync_stale_dimensions(
    52→    plan: PlanModel,
    53→    state: StateModel,
    54→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    55→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
    56→
    57→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
    58→       longer stale (the user refreshed them, or mechanical evidence changed).
    59→    2. If the queue is empty after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
    60→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    61→    """
    62→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    63→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    64→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    65→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    66→
    67→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    68→    to_remove: list[str] = [
    69→        fid for fid in order
    70→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in stale_ids
    71→    ]
    72→    for fid in to_remove:
    73→        order.remove(fid)
    74→        result.pruned.append(fid)
    75→
    76→    # --- Inject: populate when queue is empty ----------------------------
    77→    if not order and stale_ids:
    78→        existing = set(order)
    79→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
    80→            if sid not in existing:
    81→                order.append(sid)
    82→                result.injected.append(sid)
    83→
    84→    return result
    85→
    86→
    87→__all__ = [
    88→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    89→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    90→]
    91→
```

```
    50→class ScanStateContractError(ValueError):
    51→    """Raised when persisted scan state violates required runtime contracts."""

    53→
    54→def _state_subjective_assessments(
    55→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    56→) -> dict[str, object]:
    57→    """Return normalized subjective assessment store from state."""
    58→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    59→    if isinstance(assessments, dict):
    60→        return assessments
    61→    raise ScanStateContractError(
    62→        "state.subjective_assessments must be an object; rerun with a valid state file"
    63→    )
    64→
    65→
    66→def _state_lang_capabilities(
    67→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    68→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    69→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
    70→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    71→    if capabilities is None:
    72→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
    73→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
    74→        return normalized
    75→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
    76→        return capabilities
    77→    raise ScanStateContractError(
    78→        "state.lang_capabilities must be an object when present"
    79→    )

   143→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   144→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   145→    config: dict[str, object],
   146→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   147→    lang: LangRun | None,
   148→) -> LangRun | None:
   149→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   150→    if not lang:
   151→        return None
   152→
   153→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   154→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   155→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   156→        lang,
   157→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   158→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   159→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   160→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   161→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   162→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   163→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   164→        ),
   165→    )
   166→
   167→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   168→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   169→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   170→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   171→    }
   172→    return runtime_lang
   173→
   174→
   175→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   176→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   177→    *,
   178→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   179→) -> int:
   180→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   181→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   182→
   183→    reset_keys = {
   184→        key.strip()
   185→        for key in assessments
   186→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   187→    }
   188→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   189→
   190→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   191→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   192→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   193→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   194→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   195→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   196→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   197→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   198→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   199→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   200→            payload.pop("components", None)
   201→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   202→            continue
   203→        assessments[key] = {
   204→            "score": 0.0,
   205→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   206→            "assessed_at": now,
   207→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   208→            "placeholder": True,
   209→        }
   210→    return len(reset_keys)
   211→
   212→
   213→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   214→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   215→) -> int:
   216→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   217→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   218→
   219→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   220→    expired = 0
   221→    for payload in assessments.values():
   222→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   223→            continue
   224→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   225→            continue
   226→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   227→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   228→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   229→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   230→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   231→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   233→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   234→        payload.pop("components", None)
   235→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   236→        expired += 1
   237→    return expired
   238→

   240→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   241→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   242→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   243→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   244→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   245→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   246→    path = Path(args.path)
   247→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   248→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   249→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   250→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   251→        state
   252→    )
   253→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   254→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   255→            state,
   256→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   257→        )
   258→
   259→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   260→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   261→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   262→
   263→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   264→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   265→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   266→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   267→
   268→    return ScanRuntime(
   269→        args=args,
   270→        state_path=state_file,
   271→        state=state,
   272→        path=path,
   273→        config=config,
   274→        lang=lang,
   275→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   276→        profile=profile,
   277→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   278→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   279→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   280→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   281→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   286→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   287→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   288→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   289→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   290→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   291→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   292→        return findings
   293→
   294→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   295→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   296→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   297→    )
   298→    if not stale:
   299→        return findings
   300→
   301→    augmented = list(findings)
   302→    augmented.extend(stale)
   303→    for stale_finding in stale:
   304→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   305→    return augmented
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   309→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→    *,
   312→    decay_scans: int,
   313→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   314→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   315→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   316→        findings,
   317→        state=runtime.state,
   318→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   319→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   320→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→
   324→def run_scan_generation(
   325→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   326→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   327→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   328→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   329→        disable_parse_cache,
   330→        enable_parse_cache,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→    enable_file_cache()
   334→    enable_parse_cache()
   335→    try:
   336→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   337→            runtime.path,
   338→            lang=runtime.lang,
   339→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   340→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   341→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   342→                profile=runtime.profile,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→    finally:
   346→        disable_parse_cache()
   347→        disable_file_cache()
   348→
   349→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   350→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   351→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   352→    )
   353→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   354→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   355→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   356→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   357→    )
   358→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   359→        findings,
   360→        runtime,
   361→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   362→    )
   363→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   364→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   365→
   366→
   367→def merge_scan_results(
   368→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   369→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   370→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   371→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   372→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   373→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   374→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   375→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   376→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   377→
   378→    if not path_changed:
   379→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   380→    else:
   381→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   382→    prev_dim_scores = (
   383→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   387→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   388→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   389→
   390→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   391→
   392→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   393→        runtime.state,
   394→        findings,
   395→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   396→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   397→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   398→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   399→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   400→            potentials=potentials,
   401→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   402→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   403→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   404→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   405→        ),
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   409→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   410→    )
   411→    state_mod.save_state(
   412→        runtime.state,
   413→        runtime.state_path,
   414→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    # Reconcile living plan + sync stale subjective dimensions
   418→    try:
   419→        from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   420→            load_plan,
   421→            reconcile_plan_after_scan,
   422→            save_plan,
   423→            sync_stale_dimensions,
   424→        )
   425→
   426→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   427→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   428→        dirty = False
   429→
   430→        # Standard reconciliation (only if plan has user content)
   431→        if (
   432→            plan.get("queue_order")
   433→            or plan.get("overrides")
   434→            or plan.get("clusters")
   435→            or plan.get("skipped")
   436→        ):
   437→            recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, runtime.state)
   438→            if recon.changes:
   439→                dirty = True
   440→            if recon.resurfaced:
   441→                print(colorize(
   442→                    f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   443→                    "cyan",
   444→                ))
   445→
   446→        # Stale dimension sync (always — handles both cleanup and injection)
   447→        dim_sync = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state)
   448→        if dim_sync.changes:
   449→            dirty = True
   450→        if dim_sync.pruned:
   451→            print(colorize(
   452→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   453→                "cyan",
   454→            ))
   455→        if dim_sync.injected:
   456→            print(colorize(
   457→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.injected)} stale subjective dimension(s) queued for refresh.",
   458→                "cyan",
   459→            ))
   460→
   461→        if dirty:
   462→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   463→    except Exception:
   464→        pass  # Plan reconciliation is best-effort
   465→
   466→    return ScanMergeResult(
   467→        diff=diff,
   468→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   469→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   470→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   471→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   472→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   473→    )
   474→
   475→
   476→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   477→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   478→    config: dict[str, object],
   479→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   480→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   481→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   482→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   483→    )
   484→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   485→    open_findings = [
   486→        finding
   487→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   488→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   489→        ).values()
   490→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   491→    ]
   492→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   493→        open_findings,
   494→        budget=noise_budget,
   495→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   496→    )
   497→
   498→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   499→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   500→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   501→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   502→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   503→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   504→    )
   505→
   506→
   507→def persist_reminder_history(
   508→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   509→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   510→) -> None:
   511→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   512→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   513→        return
   514→
   515→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   516→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   517→    state_mod.save_state(
   518→        runtime.state,
   519→        runtime.state_path,
   520→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   521→    )
   522→
   523→
   524→__all__ = [
   525→    "ScanStateContractError",
   526→    "ScanMergeResult",
   527→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   528→    "ScanRuntime",
   529→    "merge_scan_results",
   530→    "persist_reminder_history",
   531→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   532→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   533→    "run_scan_generation",
   534→]
   535→
```

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    original_id: Required[str]
    44→    original_detector: str
    45→    original_file: str
    46→    original_summary: str
    47→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    48→    superseded_at: str
    49→    remapped_to: str | None
    50→    candidates: list[str]
    51→    note: str | None
    52→
    53→
    54→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    55→    version: Required[int]
    56→    created: Required[str]
    57→    updated: Required[str]
    58→    queue_order: list[str]
    59→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    60→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    61→    active_cluster: str | None
    62→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    63→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    64→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    65→
    66→
    67→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    68→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    69→    now = utc_now()
    70→    return {
    71→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    72→        "created": now,
    73→        "updated": now,
    74→        "queue_order": [],
    75→        "deferred": [],
    76→        "skipped": {},
    77→        "active_cluster": None,
    78→        "overrides": {},
    79→        "clusters": {},
    80→        "superseded": {},
    81→    }
    82→
    83→
    84→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    85→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    86→
    87→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    88→    """
    89→    defaults = empty_plan()
    90→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    91→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    92→
    93→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    94→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    95→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
    96→        plan["deferred"] = []
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
    98→        plan["skipped"] = {}
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   100→        plan["overrides"] = {}
```

```
1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "PLAN_FILE",
   107→    "has_living_plan",
   108→    "load_plan",
   109→    "plan_path_for_state",
   110→    "save_plan",
   111→]
   112→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_skipped: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→
    43→
    44→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    45→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    46→
    47→    items: list[dict]
    48→    total: int
    49→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    50→    requested_tier: int | None
    51→    selected_tier: int | None
    52→    fallback_reason: str | None
    53→    available_tiers: list[int]
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→
    56→
    57→def _apply_plan_order(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    plan: dict,
    60→    *,
    61→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    62→    cluster: str | None = None,
    63→) -> list[dict]:
    64→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    65→
    66→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    67→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    68→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    69→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    70→    """
    71→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    72→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    73→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    74→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    75→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    76→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    77→
    78→    # Build lookup
    79→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    80→    for item in items:
    81→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    82→
    83→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    84→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    85→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    86→        if override.get("description"):
    87→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    88→        if override.get("note"):
    89→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    90→        if override.get("cluster"):
    91→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    92→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    93→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    94→                "name": cluster_name,
    95→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    96→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    97→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    98→            }
    99→
   100→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   101→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   102→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   103→    for fid in queue_order:
   104→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   105→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   106→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   107→
   108→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   109→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   110→    for item in items:
   111→        item_id = item["id"]
   112→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   113→            continue
   114→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   115→            skipped_items.append(item)
   116→        else:
   117→            remaining.append(item)
   118→
   119→    # Assign queue positions
   120→    result = ordered + remaining
   121→    if include_skipped:
   122→        result = result + skipped_items
   123→
   124→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   136→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   137→    if effective_cluster:
   138→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   139→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   140→        if cluster_member_ids:
   141→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   142→
   143→    return result
   144→
   145→
   146→def build_work_queue(
   147→    state: StateModel,
   148→    *,
   149→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   150→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   151→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   152→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   153→
   154→    status = resolved_options.status
   155→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   156→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   157→    try:
   158→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   159→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   160→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   161→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   162→
   163→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   164→        state,
   165→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   166→        status_filter=status,
   167→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   168→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   172→    if (
   173→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   174→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   175→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   176→    ):
   177→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   178→            state,
   179→            state.get("findings", {}),
   180→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   181→        )
   182→        for item in subjective_items:
   183→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   184→                all_items.append(item)
   185→
   186→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   187→
   188→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   189→    if resolved_options.plan:
   190→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   191→            all_items,
   192→            resolved_options.plan,
   193→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   194→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   195→        )
   196→
   197→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   198→
   199→    requested_tier = (
   200→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
```

```
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(

    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low

    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:

    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        elif group == "cluster":
   182→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   183→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   184→        else:
   185→            key = "items"
   186→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   187→    return grouped
   188→
   189→
   190→__all__ = [
   191→    "build_finding_items",
   192→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   193→    "item_explain",
   194→    "item_sort_key",
   195→    "tier_counts",
   196→    "subjective_score_value",
   197→    "group_queue_items",
   198→]
   199→
```

```
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
```

Perfect! Now I have all the information. Let me provide you with a clear, comprehensive picture of the data flow:

---

## Living Plan Queue_Order Population Data Flow

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here's how `queue_order` gets populated:

### **1. When Does plan.json Get Items Added to queue_order?**

**Answer: The queue_order is NOT auto-populated from findings.** It's a **user-controlled living plan** that gets manually modified through CLI commands:

- **Manual plan commands** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/`:
  - `move` — explicitly move items to specific positions (top/bottom/before/after/up/down)
  - `describe` — add descriptions to items
  - `annotate` — add notes
  - `defer` / `undefer` — skip items temporarily
  - `focus` / `cluster` — group related items

The queue_order is read **in-memory only** during command execution (loaded from `plan.json` at runtime, not persisted as a queue of all findings).

---

### **2. Does Scan Automatically Populate the Plan with Findings?**

**Answer: NO, scan does NOT auto-populate queue_order.** The scan flow in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py` (lines 417-464):

1. **Runs reconciliation** (`reconcile_plan_after_scan()` — line 437)
   - **Removes** findings from `queue_order` when they disappear (moves them to `superseded`)
   - Does NOT add new findings to the queue

2. **Syncs stale dimensions** (`sync_stale_dimensions()` — line 447)
   - Only populates `queue_order` with **synthetic subjective IDs** when the queue is empty AND stale dimensions exist
   - Otherwise, just cleans up resolved subjective items

---

### **3. How Do stale_dimensions.py Subjective:: Items Get Added?**

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`:

**Two-phase process:**

**Phase 1 — Cleanup** (lines 67-74):
- Remove `subjective::<slug>` IDs from `queue_order` that are NO LONGER stale
- User refreshed dimensions, or mechanical evidence changed

**Phase 2 — Inject** (lines 76-83):
```python
if not order and stale_ids:  # Only if queue is empty!
    for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
        if sid not in existing:
            order.append(sid)  # Add synthetic ID
```

- **Only injects** when `queue_order` becomes empty
- Creates synthetic IDs: `subjective::{slugified_dimension_key}`
- These IDs are **NOT real findings** — they reference subjective dimension scores from `state.dimension_scores`

---

### **4. Is There Auto-Population from T1/T2/T3/T4 Findings?**

**Answer: NO.** Tier assignment is **mechanical ranking only**, not plan population:

From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`:

**Tier Assignment (line 55-56):**
```python
item["effective_tier"] = (
    4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
)
```

**Tier Hierarchy (lines 156-161):**
- **T1/T2/T3**: Mechanical findings with explicit `tier` field (set during scan by detectors)
- **T4**: All subjective findings (forced to tier 4)

**Where tiers come from:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py` — `make_finding()` creates findings with a `tier` parameter
- Detectors assign tiers (1-3) based on severity
- During work queue building, T4 items (subjective) never outrank T1/T2/T3 (line 113-114)

**Sorting within tiers** (`item_sort_key()`, lines 83-101):
```
(tier, is_subjective, confidence, review_weight, count, id)
```

This **sorts** items by tier but does NOT add them to the plan.

---

## Complete Data Flow Summary

```
SCAN WORKFLOW (scan_workflow.py:417-464)
├─ merge_scan_results()
│  └─ state_mod.merge_scan() — adds findings to state
├─ reconcile_plan_after_scan()  ← PLAN HOUSEKEEPING
│  └─ Moves disappeared findings to "superseded"
│  └─ Does NOT auto-add new findings to queue_order
└─ sync_stale_dimensions()  ← SUBJECTIVE DIMENSION SYNC
   ├─ Cleans up resolved subjective IDs
   └─ Injects subjective::<slug> IDs ONLY if queue_order is empty

WORK QUEUE BUILDING (core.py: _apply_plan_order)
├─ build_finding_items()
│  └─ Assigns tier (1-3 from finding, 4 if subjective)
├─ All items sorted by item_sort_key() (tier, confidence, etc.)
└─ _apply_plan_order()
   └─ Reorders: [items in queue_order] + [remaining sorted items] + [skipped]
   └─ Does NOT modify queue_order itself

PLAN MODIFICATION (manual only)
└─ User commands (move, defer, etc.) explicitly modify queue_order
```

---

## Key Insights

1. **queue_order is intent-only**: It's a user-curated prioritization layer, NOT a mirror of all findings
2. **Stale dimensions fill a gap**: When the plan queue is empty, subjective dimensions surface to keep users engaged
3. **Tiers enable ranking, not queuing**: T1/T2/T3/T4 are used to sort items dynamically; they don't populate the plan
4. **Reconciliation is conservative**: It only removes dead findings and cleans up stale subjective items; it never adds new findings to the queue

```
    56→            )
```

```
    60→    *,
    61→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    62→    cluster: str | None = None,
    63→) -> list[dict]:
    64→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    65→
    66→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    67→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    68→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    69→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    70→    """
    71→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    72→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    73→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    74→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    75→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    76→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    77→
    78→    # Build lookup
    79→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    80→    for item in items:
    81→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    82→
    83→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    84→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    85→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    86→        if override.get("description"):
    87→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    88→        if override.get("note"):
    89→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    90→        if override.get("cluster"):
    91→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    92→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    93→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    94→                "name": cluster_name,
    95→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    96→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    97→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    98→            }
    99→
   100→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   101→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   102→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   103→    for fid in queue_order:
   104→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   105→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   106→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   107→
   108→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   109→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   110→    for item in items:
   111→        item_id = item["id"]
   112→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   113→            continue
   114→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   115→            skipped_items.append(item)
   116→        else:
   117→            remaining.append(item)
   118→
   119→    # Assign queue positions
   120→    result = ordered + remaining
   121→    if include_skipped:
   122→        result = result + skipped_items
   123→
   124→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   136→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   137→    if effective_cluster:
   138→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   139→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   140→        if cluster_member_ids:
   141→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   142→
   143→    return result
   144→
   145→
   146→def build_work_queue(
   147→    state: StateModel,
   148→    *,
   149→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   150→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   151→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   152→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   153→
   154→    status = resolved_options.status
   155→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   156→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   157→    try:
   158→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   159→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   160→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   161→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   162→
   163→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   164→        state,
   165→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   166→        status_filter=status,
   167→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   168→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   172→    if (
   173→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   174→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   175→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   176→    ):
   177→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   178→            state,
   179→            state.get("findings", {}),
   180→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   181→        )
   182→        for item in subjective_items:
   183→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   184→                all_items.append(item)
   185→
   186→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   187→
   188→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   189→    if resolved_options.plan:
   190→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   191→            all_items,
   192→            resolved_options.plan,
   193→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   194→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   195→        )
   196→
   197→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   198→
   199→    requested_tier = (
   200→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   201→    )
   202→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   203→    fallback_reason = None
   204→    filtered = all_items
   205→
   206→    if requested_tier is not None:
   207→        filtered = [
   208→            item
   209→            for item in all_items
   210→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   211→        ]
   212→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   213→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   214→            if chosen is not None:
   215→                selected_tier = chosen
   216→                filtered = [
   217→                    item
   218→                    for item in all_items
   219→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   220→                ]
   221→                fallback_reason = (
   222→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   223→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   224→                )
   225→        elif not filtered:
   226→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   227→
   228→    total = len(filtered)
   229→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   230→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   231→
   232→    if resolved_options.explain:
   233→        for item in filtered:
   234→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   235→
   236→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   237→    return {
   238→        "items": filtered,
   239→        "total": total,
   240→        "tier_counts": counts,
   241→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   242→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   243→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   244→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   245→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   246→    }
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = [
   250→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   251→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   252→    "WorkQueueResult",
   253→    "build_work_queue",
   254→    "group_queue_items",
   255→]
   256→
```
