```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        elif group == "cluster":
   182→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   183→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   184→        else:
   185→            key = "items"
   186→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   187→    return grouped
   188→
   189→
   190→__all__ = [
   191→    "build_finding_items",
   192→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   193→    "item_explain",
   194→    "item_sort_key",
   195→    "tier_counts",
   196→    "subjective_score_value",
   197→    "group_queue_items",
   198→]
   199→
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "reset":
    77→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if plan_action == "move":
    81→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    82→
    83→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action in (
    87→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    88→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    89→    ):
    90→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    91→            cmd_plan_describe,
    92→            cmd_plan_done,
    93→            cmd_plan_focus,
    94→            cmd_plan_note,
    95→            cmd_plan_reopen,
    96→            cmd_plan_skip,
    97→            cmd_plan_unskip,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→        dispatch = {
   101→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   102→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   103→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   105→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   106→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   107→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   108→        }
   109→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   114→
   115→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   119→
   120→
   121→# Backwards-compatible alias
   122→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   123→
   124→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   125→
```

```
1→"""Sync stale subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Handles both directions:
     4→  1. **Cleanup** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale.
     5→  2. **Inject**  — when the queue is empty after cleanup, add stale dimensions.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→
    15→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    20→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    21→
    22→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→
    25→    @property
    26→    def changes(self) -> int:
    27→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    32→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    33→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    34→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    38→    if not dim_scores:
    39→        return set()
    40→
    41→    stale: set[str] = set()
    42→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    43→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    44→            continue
    45→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    46→        if dim_key:
    47→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    48→    return stale
    49→
    50→
    51→def sync_stale_dimensions(
    52→    plan: PlanModel,
    53→    state: StateModel,
    54→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    55→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
    56→
    57→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
    58→       longer stale (the user refreshed them, or mechanical evidence changed).
    59→    2. If the queue is empty after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
    60→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    61→    """
    62→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    63→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    64→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    65→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    66→
    67→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    68→    to_remove: list[str] = [
    69→        fid for fid in order
    70→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in stale_ids
    71→    ]
    72→    for fid in to_remove:
    73→        order.remove(fid)
    74→        result.pruned.append(fid)
    75→
    76→    # --- Inject: populate when queue is empty ----------------------------
    77→    if not order and stale_ids:
    78→        existing = set(order)
    79→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
    80→            if sid not in existing:
    81→                order.append(sid)
    82→                result.injected.append(sid)
    83→
    84→    return result
    85→
    86→
    87→__all__ = [
    88→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    89→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    90→]
    91→
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    15→        "id": item.get("id"),
    16→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    17→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    18→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    19→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": item.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    24→        "status": item.get("status"),
    25→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→    }
    27→    explain = item.get("explain")
    28→    if explain is not None:
    29→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    30→
    31→    # Plan metadata
    32→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    33→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    34→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    35→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    36→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    37→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    38→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    39→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    40→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    41→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    42→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    43→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    44→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    45→
    46→    return serialized
    47→
    48→
    49→def build_query_payload(
    50→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    51→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    52→    *,
    53→    command: str,
    54→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    55→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    56→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    57→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    58→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    59→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    60→        "command": command,
    61→        "items": serialized,
    62→        "queue": {
    63→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    64→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    65→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    66→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    67→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    68→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    69→        },
    70→        "narrative": narrative,
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    if plan and (
    74→        plan.get("queue_order")
    75→        or plan.get("skipped")
    76→        or plan.get("clusters")
    77→    ):
    78→        clusters_summary = []
    79→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    80→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    81→            clusters_summary.append({
    82→                "name": name,
    83→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
    84→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
    85→            })
    86→        payload["plan"] = {
    87→            "active": True,
    88→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
    89→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
    90→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
    91→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
    92→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
    93→        }
    94→
    95→    return payload
    96→
    97→
    98→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
    99→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   100→    lines = [
   101→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   102→        "",
   103→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   104→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   105→    ]
   106→    for item in items:
   107→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   108→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   109→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   110→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   111→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   112→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   113→    lines.append("")
   114→    return "\n".join(lines)
   115→
   116→
   117→def write_output_file(
   118→    output_file: str,
   119→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   120→    item_count: int,
   121→    *,
   122→    safe_write_text_fn,
   123→    colorize_fn,
   124→) -> bool:
   125→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   126→    try:
   127→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   128→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   129→    except OSError as exc:
   130→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   131→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   132→        return False
   133→    return True
   134→
   135→
   136→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   137→    output_format: str,
   138→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   139→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   140→) -> bool:
   141→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   142→    renderers = {
   143→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   144→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   145→    }
   146→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   147→    if renderer is None:
   148→        return False
   149→    renderer()
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→__all__ = [
   154→    "build_query_payload",
   155→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   156→    "render_markdown",
   157→    "serialize_item",
   158→    "write_output_file",
   159→]
   160→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()

   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)

   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(

   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}

   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (

   258→            else:

   263→            elif cli_keys:

   266→                )
   267→            else:

   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {

   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {

   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,

   292→        )
```

```
   277→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   278→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)

   290→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   291→    if queue.get("items"):
   292→        return False
   293→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   294→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))

   306→def render_terminal_items(
   307→    items: list[dict],
   308→    dim_scores: dict,
   309→    findings_scoped: dict,
   310→    *,
   311→    group: str,
   312→    explain: bool,
   313→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   314→    plan: dict | None = None,
   315→) -> None:
   316→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   317→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   318→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   319→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   320→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   321→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   322→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   323→
   324→    if group != "item":
   325→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   326→        return
   327→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   328→        if idx > 0:
   329→            print()
   330→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   331→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   332→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   333→        elif len(items) > 1:
   334→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   335→        else:
   336→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   337→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   338→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   339→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   340→
   341→
   342→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   343→    if len(items) != 1 or items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   344→        return
   345→    item = items[0]
   346→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   347→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   348→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   349→        primary = item.get(
   350→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   351→        )
   352→        print(f"    {primary}")
   353→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   354→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   355→        return
   356→
   357→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   358→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   359→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   360→        print(f"    {primary}")
   361→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   362→    else:
   363→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   364→
   365→    print(
   366→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   367→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   368→    )
   369→    print(
   370→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   371→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   372→    )
   373→
   374→
   375→def render_followup_nudges(
   376→    state: dict,
   377→    dim_scores: dict,
   378→    findings_scoped: dict,
   379→    *,
   380→    strict_score: float | None,
   381→    target_strict_score: float,
   382→) -> None:
   383→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   384→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   385→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   386→        state,
   387→        subjective_entries,
   388→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   389→        max_quality_items=3,
   390→        max_integrity_items=5,
   391→    )
   392→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   393→    if strict_score is not None:
   394→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   395→        if gap > 0:
   396→            print(
   397→                colorize(
   398→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   399→                    "cyan",
   400→                )
   401→            )
   402→        else:
   403→            print(
   404→                colorize(
   405→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   406→                    "green",
   407→                )
   408→            )
   409→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   410→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   411→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   412→
   413→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   414→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   415→        findings_scoped
   416→    )
   417→    parts: list[str] = []
   418→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   419→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   420→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   421→    open_review = [
   422→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   423→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   424→    ]
   425→    if low_dims:
   426→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   427→    if stale_count:
   428→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   429→    if unassessed_count:
   430→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   431→    if len(open_review):
   432→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   433→    if coverage_open > 0:
   434→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   435→
   436→    if parts:
   437→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   438→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   439→
   440→
   441→__all__ = [
   442→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   443→    "render_followup_nudges",
   444→    "render_queue_header",
   445→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   446→    "render_terminal_items",
   447→    "scorecard_subjective",
   448→    "show_empty_queue",
   449→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   450→]
   451→
```

## Comprehensive Analysis: `next` Command and `plan` System Integration

### 1. What does `next` do step by step?

The `next` command shows the highest-priority items from the work queue. Here's the detailed flow:

**Entry point:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py:cmd_next()`

1. **Validate state**: Check that a scan has been completed (`require_completed_scan()`)
2. **Load the living plan** (lines 93-102):
   - Load plan from disk via `load_plan()`
   - Mark `plan_data` as active if it has `queue_order`, `overrides`, or `clusters`
3. **Determine effective cluster** (lines 104-109):
   - Auto-scope to focused cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster given
4. **Build work queue** (lines 111-127): Call `build_work_queue()` with options
5. **Compute narrative**: Get coaching/strategy text via `compute_narrative()`
6. **Build query payload** (lines 137-150): Serialize for output formats and query.json
7. **Render output**: Terminal, JSON, markdown, or file output

### 2. How does the work queue get built? (`build_work_queue()`)

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:build_work_queue()`

The queue builds in this order:

**Step 1: Construct items from state findings** (lines 163-169)
- Call `build_finding_items()` → iterates `state["findings"]` and creates items
- Each finding becomes a dict with `id`, `kind: "finding"`, `effective_tier`, etc.
- Items are scoped by path and filtered by status

**Step 2: Add subjective dimensions** (lines 172-184)
- If `include_subjective=True` and status is "open" or "all"
- Call `build_subjective_items()` → creates synthetic tier-4 items with IDs like `subjective::<dimension_slug>`
- These represent stale/low-scoring dimensions below the threshold
- Example ID: `subjective::maintainability`

**Step 3: Sort mechanically** (line 186)
- Call `item_sort_key()` on all items
- Sort tuples: `(tier, subjective_flag, confidence/score, count, id)`
- Subjective items always sort as tier 4 and after mechanical items within same tier

**Step 4: Apply plan ordering** (lines 188-195)
- If `plan` is provided, call `_apply_plan_order()`
- This reorders items according to the living plan and annotates them with plan metadata

**Step 5: Calculate tier counts** (line 197)
- Count items in each tier (1, 2, 3, 4)

**Step 6: Apply tier filtering** (lines 206-227)
- If specific tier requested, filter to that tier
- If tier empty and `no_tier_fallback=False`, choose nearest non-empty tier
- Set `fallback_reason` if tier changed

**Step 7: Limit to count** (lines 229-230)
- Slice to requested item count (default: 1)

**Step 8: Add explain if needed** (lines 232-234)
- Compute `item_explain()` dict for each item

### 3. How does `_apply_plan_order()` work?

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:_apply_plan_order()` (lines 57-143)

This function reorders and annotates items based on the living plan. Here's the algorithm:

```
1. Build lookup: by_id[item["id"]] = item

2. Annotate all items with plan metadata:
   - Add plan_description from overrides[item_id]["description"]
   - Add plan_note from overrides[item_id]["note"]
   - Add plan_cluster info from overrides[item_id]["cluster"]

3. Split items into three groups:
   a) ordered: Items in plan["queue_order"] (in that order)
   b) remaining: Items NOT in queue_order and NOT skipped
   c) skipped_items: Items in plan["skipped"]

4. Combine: result = ordered + remaining
   (skipped_items appended last if include_skipped=True)

5. Annotate queue positions: item["queue_position"] = pos + 1

6. Mark skipped items with metadata:
   - plan_skipped = True
   - plan_skip_kind = "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
   - plan_skip_reason = reason text

7. Filter to cluster if requested:
   - If cluster is set, keep only items in that cluster
   - Cluster membership is defined by plan["clusters"][cluster_name]["finding_ids"]
```

**Key insight**: Plan ordering prepends items to the queue but doesn't remove other items—it just reorders them!

### 4. Plan queue_order vs tier-based ordering

These work together in a **hierarchical** way:

| Level | Mechanism | Controlled by |
|-------|-----------|---------------|
| **1st** | Manual plan order | `plan["queue_order"]` list |
| **2nd** | Mechanical tier sort | `item_sort_key()` within remaining items |
| **3rd** | Tier filtering/fallback | Requested tier + fallback logic |

**Example flow**:
- Plan specifies: `queue_order = ["finding_A", "finding_B"]`
- State has: `finding_A` (T3), `finding_B` (T2), `finding_C` (T1), `finding_D` (T1)
- Mechanical sort of remaining: `finding_D`, `finding_C` (both T1)
- **Result order**: `finding_A`, `finding_B`, `finding_D`, `finding_C`

### 5. Relationship between plan and work queue

**Data flow**:
```
plan.json
  ├── queue_order[]        → prepends items to queue (highest priority)
  ├── skipped{}            → marks items for demotion/hiding
  ├── overrides{}          → enriches items with descriptions/notes
  └── clusters{}           → groups items; active_cluster filters to subset
         └── finding_ids[]

Work Queue Building:
  1. findings from state + synthetic subjective items
  2. mechanical sort by item_sort_key()
  3. _apply_plan_order() reorders + annotates
  4. tier filtering + limit to count
  5. serialized to output
```

### 6. Where do items come from when running `next`?

**Sources** (in order of construction):

1. **Real findings** from `state["findings"]` (filtered by scan_path, status)
   - These have real IDs like `python::undefined_name::abc123def456`
   - Detectors: `undefined_name`, `unused_import`, `review`, `subjective_assessment`, `holistic_review`, etc.

2. **Synthetic subjective items** (if threshold allows)
   - IDs: `subjective::<dimension_slug>` (e.g., `subjective::maintainability`)
   - Created by `build_subjective_items()` when dimension scores below threshold
   - Always tier 4, never outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3

3. **Plan-ordered items**
   - Any items referenced in `plan["queue_order"]` appear first
   - Can be real findings OR synthetic subjective IDs

### 7. Subjective synthetic IDs: `subjective::` prefix

**Purpose**: Represent stale or low-scoring subjective dimensions as actionable work items

**ID structure**: `subjective::<slugified_dimension_key>`

Examples:
- `subjective::maintainability` → for "Maintainability" dimension
- `subjective::code_elegance` → for "Code elegance" dimension

**Lifecycle** (from `stale_dimensions.py`):

1. **Injection** (lines 76-82): When queue is empty after cleanup, inject all stale dimension IDs
2. **Cleanup** (lines 67-74): Remove `subjective::*` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale

**Stale determination** (from `_current_stale_ids()`):
- A dimension is stale if `scorecard_subjective_entries()[entry]["stale"] == True`
- Stale means: dimension was reviewed recently but hasn't been re-assessed with new findings

**Item construction** (from `build_subjective_items()` lines 271-293):
```python
{
    "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
    "detector": "subjective_assessment",
    "kind": "subjective_dimension",
    "tier": 4,
    "effective_tier": 4,
    "confidence": "medium",
    "summary": f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({score:.1f}%)",
    "detail": {
        "dimension_name": "Maintainability",  # display name
        "dimension": "maintainability",       # key
        "strict_score": 72.3,
        "issues": 5,
        "stale": True/False,
        ...
    },
    "primary_command": "desloppify review --prepare [--dimensions ...]",
    ...
}
```

### 8. How are subjective items different from real findings?

| Aspect | Real Finding | Subjective Dimension |
|--------|--------------|---------------------|
| **ID** | `<detector>::<file>::<hash>` | `subjective::<dimension_slug>` |
| **Kind** | `"finding"` | `"subjective_dimension"` |
| **Source** | State findings, parsed from code | Scorecard, computed from all findings in dimension |
| **Tier** | T1, T2, T3 (mechanical) | Always T4 (fixed) |
| **Detector** | `undefined_name`, `unused_import`, `review`, etc. | `subjective_assessment` |
| **Score basis** | Code evidence | User assessment + evidence count |
| **Outranking** | T1 always beats T2/T3 | Never outranks mechanical T1/T2/T3 |
| **Synthetic?** | No (real code issues) | **Yes** (synthetic queue items) |

### 9. `plan show` vs `plan next` vs `plan`

**Dispatch table** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py`:

| Command | Function | Purpose |
|---------|----------|---------|
| `desloppify plan` (no subcommand) | `_cmd_plan_generate()` | Generate **prioritized markdown** breaking down all findings by tier, with dimension health table |
| `desloppify plan show` | `_cmd_plan_show()` | Show **plan metadata summary** (counts: ordered items, skipped, clusters, overrides, active cluster) |
| `desloppify plan next` | ❌ **Does NOT exist** | — |
| `desloppify next` | `cmd_next()` | Show **highest-priority items** (1 by default) with detailed rendering, narrative, dimension scores |

**Key differences**:
- `plan`: Holistic view of all tiers + plan state
- `plan show`: Metadata about plan structure (no findings)
- `next`: Single-item detail view with coaching context

### 10. Complete information flow diagram

```
User runs: desloppify next --tier 2 --count 3

    ↓

cmd_next() {
    load_plan()  ← read .desloppify/plan.json
    
    build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            tier=2,
            count=3,
            include_subjective=True,
            subjective_threshold=95.0,
            plan=plan_data
        )
    ) {
        # Build finding items from state
        finding_items = build_finding_items(state)
        
        # Add synthetic subjective items
        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(state, threshold=95.0)
        
        all_items = finding_items + subjective_items
        
        # Sort mechanically
        all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
        
        # Apply plan ordering
        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
            all_items,
            plan,
            include_skipped=False,
            cluster=None
        ) {
            # Reorder: plan.queue_order items first, then remaining
            ordered + remaining
            
            # Annotate with plan metadata
            # Filter to cluster if active_cluster set
        }
        
        # Count tiers
        counts = {1: 10, 2: 5, 3: 3, 4: 20}
        
        # Filter to T2
        filtered = [item for item in all_items if effective_tier == 2]
        
        # Limit to count
        filtered = filtered[:3]
        
        return {
            "items": filtered,
            "tier_counts": counts,
            "selected_tier": 2,
            ...
        }
    }
    
    render_terminal_items(items)  ← pretty print
    
    write_query(payload)  ← write .desloppify/query.json
}

    ↓

Output:
  - Terminal: colored item details with plan annotations
  - query.json: serialized payload for integrations
```

---

## Key Implementation Details

### How `item_sort_key()` works (ranking.py:83-101)

**For subjective items** (line 84-90):
```python
return (
    int(effective_tier),        # T4 for all subjective
    1,                          # Sort after mechanical within tier
    subjective_score_value(),   # Lowest score first
    id                          # Tiebreaker
)
```

**For real findings** (line 93-101):
```python
return (
    effective_tier,                     # T1 < T2 < T3
    0,                                  # Sort before subjective within tier
    CONFIDENCE_ORDER[confidence],       # high < medium < low
    -review_weight,                     # Highest weight first (review findings)
    -count,                             # Most issues first
    id                                  # Tiebreaker
)
```

### How subjective items are created (helpers.py:198-293)

**Input**: Scorecard subjective entries from `state["dimension_scores"]`

**Logic**:
1. Loop over entries where `strict_score < threshold` (default 95%)
2. Check if dimension is unassessed, stale, or has open review findings
3. Determine primary command based on state:
   - `desloppify review --prepare` if unassessed or stale
   - `desloppify show review --status open` if has open review findings
4. Create synthetic item with `kind: "subjective_dimension"`

### Plan persistence (schema.py)

**File**: `.desloppify/plan.json`

**Structure**:
```json
{
    "version": 2,
    "created": "2025-02-27T...",
    "updated": "2025-02-27T...",
    "queue_order": ["finding_A", "subjective::maintainability", "finding_B"],
    "deferred": [],  // legacy, kept empty
    "skipped": {
        "finding_C": {
            "kind": "temporary",
            "reason": "waiting for refactor",
            "review_after": 2  // resurface after 2 scans
        }
    },
    "active_cluster": "core_logic",
    "overrides": {
        "finding_A": {
            "description": "Critical: blocks 3 other features",
            "note": "assigned to alice",
            "cluster": "core_logic"
        }
    },
    "clusters": {
        "core_logic": {
            "description": "Core interpreter logic",
            "finding_ids": ["finding_A", "finding_B", ...]
        }
    },
    "superseded": { /* archived */ }
}
```

---

**Summary**: The `next` command orchestrates a sophisticated priority queue that merges **mechanical tier-based ranking**, **synthetic subjective dimensions**, and **user-driven plan overrides** into a unified work queue. The plan acts as a behavioral layer on top of the mechanical ranking, allowing users to manually reorder items and group them into clusters while preserving the underlying tier system as a safety net.

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass

     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (

    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_skipped: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→
    43→
    44→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    45→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    46→
    47→    items: list[dict]
    48→    total: int
    49→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    50→    requested_tier: int | None
    51→    selected_tier: int | None
    52→    fallback_reason: str | None
    53→    available_tiers: list[int]
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→
    56→
    57→def _apply_plan_order(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    plan: dict,
    60→    *,
    61→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    62→    cluster: str | None = None,
    63→) -> list[dict]:
    64→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    65→
    66→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    67→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    68→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    69→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    70→    """
    71→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    72→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    73→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    74→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    75→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    76→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    77→
    78→    # Build lookup
    79→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    80→    for item in items:
    81→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    82→
    83→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    84→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    85→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    86→        if override.get("description"):
    87→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    88→        if override.get("note"):
    89→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    90→        if override.get("cluster"):
    91→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    92→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    93→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    94→                "name": cluster_name,
    95→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    96→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    97→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    98→            }
    99→
   100→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   101→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   102→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   103→    for fid in queue_order:
   104→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   105→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   106→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   107→
   108→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   109→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   110→    for item in items:
   111→        item_id = item["id"]
   112→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   113→            continue
   114→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   115→            skipped_items.append(item)
   116→        else:
   117→            remaining.append(item)
   118→
   119→    # Assign queue positions
   120→    result = ordered + remaining
   121→    if include_skipped:
   122→        result = result + skipped_items
   123→
   124→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   136→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   137→    if effective_cluster:
   138→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   139→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   140→        if cluster_member_ids:
   141→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   142→
   143→    return result
   144→
   145→
   146→def build_work_queue(
   147→    state: StateModel,
   148→    *,
   149→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   150→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   151→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   152→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   153→
   154→    status = resolved_options.status
   155→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   156→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   157→    try:
   158→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   159→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   160→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   161→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   162→
   163→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   164→        state,
   165→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   166→        status_filter=status,
   167→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   168→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   172→    if (
   173→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   174→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   175→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   176→    ):
   177→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   178→            state,
   179→            state.get("findings", {}),
   180→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   181→        )
   182→        for item in subjective_items:
   183→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   184→                all_items.append(item)
   185→
   186→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   187→
   188→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   189→    if resolved_options.plan:
   190→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   191→            all_items,
   192→            resolved_options.plan,
   193→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   194→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   195→        )
   196→
   197→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   198→
   199→    requested_tier = (
   200→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   201→    )
   202→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   203→    fallback_reason = None
   204→    filtered = all_items
   205→
   206→    if requested_tier is not None:
   207→        filtered = [
   208→            item
   209→            for item in all_items
   210→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   211→        ]
   212→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   213→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   214→            if chosen is not None:
   215→                selected_tier = chosen
   216→                filtered = [
   217→                    item
   218→                    for item in all_items
   219→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   220→                ]
   221→                fallback_reason = (
   222→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   223→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   224→                )
   225→        elif not filtered:
   226→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   227→
   228→    total = len(filtered)
   229→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   230→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   231→
   232→    if resolved_options.explain:
   233→        for item in filtered:
   234→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   235→
   236→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   237→    return {
   238→        "items": filtered,
   239→        "total": total,
   240→        "tier_counts": counts,
   241→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   242→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   243→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   244→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   245→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   246→    }
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = [
   250→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   251→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   252→    "WorkQueueResult",
   253→    "build_work_queue",
   254→    "group_queue_items",
   255→]
   256→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        elif group == "cluster":
   182→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   183→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   184→        else:
   185→            key = "items"
   186→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   187→    return grouped
   188→
   189→
   190→__all__ = [
   191→    "build_finding_items",
   192→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   193→    "item_explain",
   194→    "item_sort_key",
   195→    "tier_counts",
   196→    "subjective_score_value",
   197→    "group_queue_items",
   198→]
   199→
```
