1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→__all__ = [
   297→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   298→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   299→    "build_subjective_items",
   300→    "detail_dict",
   301→    "is_review_finding",
   302→    "is_subjective_finding",
   303→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   304→    "review_finding_weight",

   306→    "slugify",
   307→    "status_matches",
   308→    "subjective_strict_scores",

   310→]

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    89→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    90→
    91→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    92→
    93→    # Load the living plan
    94→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    95→    plan = load_plan()
    96→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    97→    if (
    98→        plan.get("queue_order")
    99→        or plan.get("overrides")
   100→        or plan.get("clusters")
   101→    ):
   102→        plan_data = plan
   103→
   104→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   105→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   106→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   107→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   108→        if active_cluster:
   109→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   110→
   111→    queue = build_work_queue(
   112→        state,
   113→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   114→            tier=tier,
   115→            count=count,
   116→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   117→            scope=scope,
   118→            status=status,
   119→            include_subjective=True,
   120→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   121→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   122→            explain=explain,
   123→            plan=plan_data,
   124→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   125→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   126→        ),
   127→    )
   128→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   129→
   130→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   131→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   132→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   133→        state,
   134→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   138→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   139→    )
   140→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   141→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   142→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   143→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   144→        state,
   145→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   146→    )
   147→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   148→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   149→    ]
   150→    write_query(payload)
   151→
   152→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   153→    if output_file:
   154→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   155→            output_file,
   156→            payload,
   157→            len(items),
   158→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   159→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   160→        ):
   161→            return
   162→        raise SystemExit(1)
   163→
   164→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   168→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   169→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   170→        return
   171→
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   173→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   174→        state.get("findings", {}),
   175→        state.get("scan_path"),
   176→    )
   177→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   178→    try:
   179→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   180→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   181→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   182→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   183→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   184→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   185→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   186→    )
   187→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   188→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   189→        state,
   190→        dim_scores,
   191→        findings_scoped,
   192→        strict_score=strict_score,
   193→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   194→    )
   195→    print()
   196→
   197→
   198→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   199→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    15→        "id": item.get("id"),
    16→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    17→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    18→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    19→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": item.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    24→        "status": item.get("status"),
    25→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→    }
    27→    explain = item.get("explain")
    28→    if explain is not None:
    29→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    30→
    31→    # Plan metadata
    32→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    33→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    34→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    35→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    36→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    37→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    38→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    39→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    40→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    41→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    42→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    43→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    44→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    45→
    46→    return serialized
    47→
    48→
    49→def build_query_payload(
    50→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    51→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    52→    *,
    53→    command: str,
    54→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    55→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    56→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    57→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    58→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    59→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    60→        "command": command,
    61→        "items": serialized,
    62→        "queue": {
    63→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    64→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    65→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    66→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    67→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    68→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    69→        },
    70→        "narrative": narrative,
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    if plan and (
    74→        plan.get("queue_order")
    75→        or plan.get("skipped")
    76→        or plan.get("clusters")
    77→    ):
    78→        clusters_summary = []
    79→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    80→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    81→            clusters_summary.append({
    82→                "name": name,
    83→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
    84→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
    85→            })
    86→        payload["plan"] = {
    87→            "active": True,
    88→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
    89→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
    90→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
    91→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
    92→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
    93→        }
    94→
    95→    return payload
    96→
    97→
    98→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
    99→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   100→    lines = [
   101→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   102→        "",
   103→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   104→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   105→    ]
   106→    for item in items:
   107→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   108→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   109→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   110→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   111→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   112→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   113→    lines.append("")
   114→    return "\n".join(lines)
   115→
   116→
   117→def write_output_file(
   118→    output_file: str,
   119→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   120→    item_count: int,
   121→    *,
   122→    safe_write_text_fn,
   123→    colorize_fn,
   124→) -> bool:
   125→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   126→    try:
   127→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   128→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   129→    except OSError as exc:
   130→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   131→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   132→        return False
   133→    return True
   134→
   135→
   136→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   137→    output_format: str,
   138→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   139→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   140→) -> bool:
   141→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   142→    renderers = {
   143→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   144→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   145→    }
   146→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   147→    if renderer is None:
   148→        return False
   149→    renderer()
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→__all__ = [
   154→    "build_query_payload",
   155→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   156→    "render_markdown",
   157→    "serialize_item",
   158→    "write_output_file",
   159→]
   160→
```

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_subjective import (
     7→    build_subjective_followup,
     8→)
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    10→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE, group_queue_items
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    subjective_review_open_breakdown,
    16→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    19→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    20→
    21→
    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    27→    if not dim_scores:
    28→        return []
    29→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    30→        state,
    31→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    32→    )
    33→
    34→
    35→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    36→    findings_scoped: dict,
    37→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    39→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    40→
    41→
    42→def _tier_label(tier: int) -> str:
    43→    return f"T{tier}"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _render_tier_navigator(queue: dict) -> None:
    47→    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    48→    print(colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold"))
    49→    print(
    50→        colorize(
    51→            f"    Open: T1:{counts.get(1, 0)} T2:{counts.get(2, 0)} "
    52→            f"T3:{counts.get(3, 0)} T4:{counts.get(4, 0)}",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(
    58→            "    Switch: `desloppify next --tier 1` | `desloppify next --tier 2` | "
    59→            "`desloppify next --tier 3` | `desloppify next --tier 4`",
    60→            "dim",
    61→        )
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    72→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    73→            print(
    74→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    75→            )
    76→
    77→
    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    81→
    82→
    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    84→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    85→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    86→        return "[review]"
    87→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    88→        return "[auto]"
    89→    return ""
    90→
    91→
    92→def _render_item(
    93→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
    94→    potentials: dict | None = None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    97→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    98→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
    99→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   100→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   101→
   102→    # Effort/type label
   103→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   104→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   105→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   106→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   107→
   108→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   109→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   110→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   111→        subjective_score = float(
   112→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   113→        )
   114→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   115→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   116→        print(
   117→            colorize(
   118→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   119→                "cyan",
   120→            )
   121→        )
   122→        print(colorize(
   123→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   124→            "dim",

   188→            if impact > 0:

   190→            else:
   191→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   192→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   193→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:

   202→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):

   204→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:

   276→
   277→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   278→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   279→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   280→        return
   281→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   282→    if not explain:
   283→        return
   284→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   285→    if available:
   286→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   287→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   288→
   289→
   290→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   291→    if queue.get("items"):
   292→        return False
   293→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   294→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   295→    if tier is not None:
   296→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   297→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   298→        if available:
   299→            commands = " | ".join(
   300→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   301→            )
   302→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   303→    return True
   304→
   305→
   306→def render_terminal_items(
   307→    items: list[dict],
   308→    dim_scores: dict,
   309→    findings_scoped: dict,
   310→    *,
   311→    group: str,
   312→    explain: bool,
   313→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   314→    plan: dict | None = None,
   315→) -> None:
   316→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   317→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   318→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   319→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   320→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   321→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   322→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   323→
   324→    if group != "item":
   325→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   326→        return
   327→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   328→        if idx > 0:
   329→            print()
   330→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   331→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   332→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   333→        elif len(items) > 1:
   334→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   335→        else:
   336→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   337→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   338→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   339→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   340→
   341→
   342→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   343→    if len(items) != 1 or items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   344→        return
   345→    item = items[0]
   346→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   347→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   348→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   349→        primary = item.get(
   350→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   351→        )
   352→        print(f"    {primary}")
   353→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   354→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   355→        return
   356→
   357→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   358→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   359→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   360→        print(f"    {primary}")
   361→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   362→    else:
   363→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   364→
   365→    print(
   366→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   367→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   368→    )
   369→    print(
   370→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   371→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   372→    )
   373→
   374→
   375→def render_followup_nudges(
   376→    state: dict,
   377→    dim_scores: dict,
   378→    findings_scoped: dict,
   379→    *,
   380→    strict_score: float | None,
   381→    target_strict_score: float,
   382→) -> None:
   383→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   384→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   385→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   386→        state,
   387→        subjective_entries,
   388→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   389→        max_quality_items=3,
   390→        max_integrity_items=5,
   391→    )
   392→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   393→    if strict_score is not None:
   394→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   395→        if gap > 0:
   396→            print(
   397→                colorize(
   398→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   399→                    "cyan",
   400→                )
   401→            )
   402→        else:
   403→            print(
   404→                colorize(
   405→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   406→                    "green",
   407→                )
   408→            )
   409→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   410→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   411→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   412→
   413→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   414→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   415→        findings_scoped
   416→    )
   417→    parts: list[str] = []
   418→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   419→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   420→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   421→    open_review = [
   422→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   423→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   424→    ]
   425→    if low_dims:
   426→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   427→    if stale_count:
   428→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   429→    if unassessed_count:
   430→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   431→    if len(open_review):
   432→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   433→    if coverage_open > 0:
   434→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   435→
   436→    if parts:
   437→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   438→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   439→
   440→
   441→__all__ = [
   442→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   443→    "render_followup_nudges",
   444→    "render_queue_header",
   445→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   446→    "render_terminal_items",
   447→    "scorecard_subjective",
   448→    "show_empty_queue",
   449→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   450→]
   451→

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    original_id: Required[str]
    44→    original_detector: str
    45→    original_file: str
    46→    original_summary: str
    47→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    48→    superseded_at: str
    49→    remapped_to: str | None
    50→    candidates: list[str]
    51→    note: str | None
    52→
    53→
    54→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    55→    version: Required[int]
    56→    created: Required[str]
    57→    updated: Required[str]
    58→    queue_order: list[str]
    59→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    60→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    61→    active_cluster: str | None
    62→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    63→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    64→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    65→
    66→
    67→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    68→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    69→    now = utc_now()
    70→    return {
    71→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    72→        "created": now,
    73→        "updated": now,
    74→        "queue_order": [],
    75→        "deferred": [],
    76→        "skipped": {},
    77→        "active_cluster": None,
    78→        "overrides": {},
    79→        "clusters": {},
    80→        "superseded": {},
    81→    }
    82→
    83→
    84→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    85→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    86→
    87→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    88→    """
    89→    defaults = empty_plan()
    90→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    91→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    92→
    93→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    94→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    95→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
    96→        plan["deferred"] = []
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
    98→        plan["skipped"] = {}
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   100→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   102→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   104→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   105→
   106→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   107→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   108→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   109→    if deferred:
   110→        now = utc_now()
   111→        for fid in list(deferred):
   112→            if fid not in skipped:
   113→                skipped[fid] = {
   114→                    "finding_id": fid,
   115→                    "kind": "temporary",
   116→                    "reason": None,
   117→                    "note": None,
   118→                    "attestation": None,
   119→                    "created_at": now,
   120→                    "review_after": None,
   121→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   122→                }
   123→        deferred.clear()
   124→
   125→    # Normalize cluster finding_ids
   126→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   127→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   128→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   129→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   130→
   131→
   132→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   133→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   134→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   135→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   136→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   137→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   138→
   139→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   140→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   141→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   142→    if overlap:
   143→        raise ValueError(
   144→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   145→        )
   146→
   147→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   148→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   149→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   150→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   151→            raise ValueError(
   152→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   153→            )
   154→
   155→
   156→__all__ = [
   157→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   158→    "Cluster",
   159→    "ItemOverride",
   160→    "PlanModel",
   161→    "SkipEntry",
   162→    "SupersededEntry",
   163→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   164→    "empty_plan",
   165→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   166→    "validate_plan",
   167→]
   168→

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel, name: str, description: str | None = None
   248→) -> Cluster:
   249→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   250→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   251→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   252→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   253→    now = utc_now()
   254→    cluster: Cluster = {
   255→        "name": name,
   256→        "description": description,
   257→        "finding_ids": [],
   258→        "created_at": now,
   259→        "updated_at": now,
   260→    }
   261→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   262→    return cluster
   263→
   264→
   265→def add_to_cluster(
   266→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   267→) -> int:
   268→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   269→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   270→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   271→    if cluster is None:
   272→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   273→
   274→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   275→    count = 0
   276→    now = utc_now()
   277→    for fid in finding_ids:
   278→        if fid not in member_ids:
   279→            member_ids.append(fid)
   280→            count += 1
   281→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   282→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   283→        if fid not in overrides:
   284→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   285→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   286→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   287→
   288→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   289→    return count
   290→
   291→
   292→def remove_from_cluster(
   293→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   294→) -> int:
   295→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   296→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   297→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   298→    if cluster is None:
   299→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   300→
   301→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   302→    now = utc_now()
   303→    count = 0
   304→    for fid in finding_ids:
   305→        if fid in member_ids:
   306→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   307→            count += 1
   308→        # Clear cluster from override
   309→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   310→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   311→            override["cluster"] = None
   312→            override["updated_at"] = now
   313→
   314→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   315→    return count
   316→
   317→
   318→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   319→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   320→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   321→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   322→    if cluster is None:
   323→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   324→
   325→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   326→    now = utc_now()
   327→    for fid in orphaned:
   328→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   329→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   330→            override["cluster"] = None
   331→            override["updated_at"] = now
   332→
   333→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   334→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   335→
   336→    return orphaned
   337→
   338→
   339→def move_cluster(
   340→    plan: PlanModel,
   341→    cluster_name: str,
   342→    position: str,
   343→    target: str | None = None,
   344→    offset: int | None = None,
   345→) -> int:
   346→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   347→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   348→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   349→    if cluster is None:
   350→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   351→
   352→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   353→    if not member_ids:
   354→        return 0
   355→
   356→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   357→
   358→
   359→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   360→# Focus
   361→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   362→
   363→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   364→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   365→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   366→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   367→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   368→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   369→
   370→
   371→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   372→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   373→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   374→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   375→
   376→
   377→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   378→# Reset
   379→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   380→
   381→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   382→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   383→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   384→    plan.clear()
   385→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   386→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   387→        plan[k] = v
   388→    plan["created"] = created
   389→
   390→
   391→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   392→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   393→
   394→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   395→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   396→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   397→    """
   398→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   399→    found = 0
   400→
   401→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   402→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   403→    for fid in finding_ids:
   404→        was_present = False
   405→        if fid in order:
   406→            order.remove(fid)
   407→            was_present = True
   408→        if fid in skipped:
   409→            skipped.pop(fid)
   410→            was_present = True
   411→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   412→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   413→            if fid in ids:
   414→                ids.remove(fid)
   415→                was_present = True
   416→        if was_present:
   417→            found += 1
   418→
   419→    return found
   420→
   421→
   422→__all__ = [
   423→    "add_to_cluster",
   424→    "annotate_finding",
   425→    "clear_focus",
   426→    "create_cluster",
   427→    "delete_cluster",
   428→    "describe_finding",
   429→    "move_cluster",
   430→    "move_items",
   431→    "purge_ids",
   432→    "remove_from_cluster",
   433→    "reset_plan",
   434→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   435→    "set_focus",
   436→    "skip_items",
   437→    "unskip_items",
   438→]
   439→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→
   147→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   148→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   149→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   150→    referenced_ids = {
   151→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   152→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   153→    }
   154→
   155→    # Check each referenced ID
   156→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   157→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   158→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   159→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   160→                result.changes += 1
   161→
   162→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   163→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   164→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   165→
   166→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   167→    if resurfaced:
   168→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   169→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   170→
   171→    # Prune old superseded entries
   172→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   173→    result.pruned = pruned
   174→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   175→
   176→    return result
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "ReconcileResult",
   181→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   182→]
   183→
```

1→"""Sync stale subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Handles both directions:
     4→  1. **Cleanup** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale.
     5→  2. **Inject**  — when the queue is empty after cleanup, add stale dimensions.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→
    15→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    20→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    21→
    22→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→
    25→    @property
    26→    def changes(self) -> int:
    27→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    32→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    33→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    34→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    38→    if not dim_scores:
    39→        return set()
    40→
    41→    stale: set[str] = set()
    42→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    43→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    44→            continue
    45→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    46→        if dim_key:
    47→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    48→    return stale
    49→
    50→
    51→def sync_stale_dimensions(
    52→    plan: PlanModel,
    53→    state: StateModel,
    54→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    55→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
    56→
    57→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
    58→       longer stale (the user refreshed them, or mechanical evidence changed).
    59→    2. If the queue is empty after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
    60→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    61→    """
    62→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    63→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    64→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    65→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    66→
    67→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    68→    to_remove: list[str] = [
    69→        fid for fid in order
    70→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in stale_ids
    71→    ]
    72→    for fid in to_remove:
    73→        order.remove(fid)
    74→        result.pruned.append(fid)
    75→
    76→    # --- Inject: populate when queue is empty ----------------------------
    77→    if not order and stale_ids:
    78→        existing = set(order)
    79→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
    80→            if sid not in existing:
    81→                order.append(sid)
    82→                result.injected.append(sid)
    83→
    84→    return result
    85→
    86→
    87→__all__ = [
    88→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    89→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    90→]
    91→
```

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "PLAN_FILE",
   107→    "has_living_plan",
   108→    "load_plan",
   109→    "plan_path_for_state",
   110→    "save_plan",
   111→]
   112→
```
