```
1→"""Plan cluster subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    add_to_cluster,
    13→    create_cluster,
    14→    delete_cluster,
    15→    load_plan,
    16→    move_cluster,
    17→    remove_from_cluster,
    18→    save_plan,
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    plan = load_plan()
    26→    try:
    27→        create_cluster(plan, name, description)
    28→    except ValueError as ex:
    29→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    30→        return
    31→    save_plan(plan)
    32→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    36→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    37→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    38→        return
    39→
    40→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    41→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    42→
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    45→    if not finding_ids:
    46→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    47→        return
    48→    try:
    49→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    50→    except ValueError as ex:
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    52→        return
    53→    save_plan(plan)
    54→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    59→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    63→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    64→
    65→    plan = load_plan()
    66→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    67→    if not finding_ids:
    68→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    69→        return
    70→    try:
    71→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    72→    except ValueError as ex:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    74→        return
    75→    save_plan(plan)
    76→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    77→
    78→
    79→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    80→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    81→    plan = load_plan()
    82→    try:
    83→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
    84→    except ValueError as ex:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    86→        return
    87→    save_plan(plan)
    88→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
    89→
    90→
    91→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    92→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    93→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    94→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    95→
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→
    98→    offset: int | None = None
    99→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   100→        try:
   101→            offset = int(target)
   102→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   103→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   104→            return
   105→        target = None
   106→
   107→    try:
   108→        count = move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   109→    except ValueError as ex:
   110→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   111→        return
   112→    save_plan(plan)
   113→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   114→
   115→
   116→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   117→    plan = load_plan()
   118→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→    if not clusters:
   121→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   122→        return
   123→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   124→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   125→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   127→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   128→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   129→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   130→
   131→
   132→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   133→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   134→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   135→    dispatch = {
   136→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   137→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   138→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   139→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   140→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   141→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   142→    }
   143→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   144→    if handler is None:
   145→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   146→        return
   147→    handler(args)
   148→
   149→
   150→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   151→
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "reset":
    77→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if plan_action == "move":
    81→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    82→
    83→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action in (
    87→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    88→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    89→    ):
    90→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    91→            cmd_plan_describe,
    92→            cmd_plan_done,
    93→            cmd_plan_focus,
    94→            cmd_plan_note,
    95→            cmd_plan_reopen,
    96→            cmd_plan_skip,
    97→            cmd_plan_unskip,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→        dispatch = {
   101→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   102→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   103→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   105→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   106→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   107→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   108→        }
   109→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   114→
   115→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   119→
   120→
   121→# Backwards-compatible alias
   122→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   123→
   124→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_skipped: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→
    43→
    44→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    45→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    46→
    47→    items: list[dict]
    48→    total: int
    49→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    50→    requested_tier: int | None
    51→    selected_tier: int | None
    52→    fallback_reason: str | None
    53→    available_tiers: list[int]
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→
    56→
    57→def _apply_plan_order(
    58→    items: list[dict],
    59→    plan: dict,
    60→    *,
    61→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    62→    cluster: str | None = None,
    63→) -> list[dict]:
    64→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    65→
    66→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    67→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    68→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    69→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    70→    """
    71→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    72→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    73→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    74→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    75→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    76→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    77→
    78→    # Build lookup
    79→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    80→    for item in items:
    81→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    82→
    83→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    84→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    85→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    86→        if override.get("description"):
    87→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    88→        if override.get("note"):
    89→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    90→        if override.get("cluster"):
    91→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    92→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    93→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    94→                "name": cluster_name,
    95→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    96→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    97→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    98→            }
    99→
   100→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   101→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   102→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   103→    for fid in queue_order:
   104→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   105→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   106→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   107→
   108→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   109→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   110→    for item in items:
   111→        item_id = item["id"]
   112→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   113→            continue
   114→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   115→            skipped_items.append(item)
   116→        else:
   117→            remaining.append(item)
   118→
   119→    # Assign queue positions
   120→    result = ordered + remaining
   121→    if include_skipped:
   122→        result = result + skipped_items
   123→
   124→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   125→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   126→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   127→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   128→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   129→            if skip_entry:
   130→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   131→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   132→                if skip_reason:
   133→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   134→
   135→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   136→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   137→    if effective_cluster:
   138→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   139→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   140→        if cluster_member_ids:
   141→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   142→
   143→    return result
   144→
   145→
   146→def build_work_queue(
   147→    state: StateModel,
   148→    *,
   149→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   150→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   151→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   152→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   153→
   154→    status = resolved_options.status
   155→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   156→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   157→    try:
   158→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   159→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   160→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   161→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   162→
   163→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   164→        state,
   165→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   166→        status_filter=status,
   167→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   168→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   172→    if (
   173→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   174→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   175→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   176→    ):
   177→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   178→            state,
   179→            state.get("findings", {}),
   180→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   181→        )
   182→        for item in subjective_items:
   183→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   184→                all_items.append(item)
   185→
   186→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   187→
   188→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   189→    if resolved_options.plan:
   190→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   191→            all_items,
   192→            resolved_options.plan,
   193→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   194→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   195→        )
   196→
   197→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   198→
   199→    requested_tier = (
   200→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   201→    )
   202→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   203→    fallback_reason = None
   204→    filtered = all_items
   205→
   206→    if requested_tier is not None:
   207→        filtered = [
   208→            item
   209→            for item in all_items
   210→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   211→        ]
   212→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   213→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   214→            if chosen is not None:
   215→                selected_tier = chosen
   216→                filtered = [
   217→                    item
   218→                    for item in all_items
   219→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   220→                ]
   221→                fallback_reason = (
   222→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   223→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   224→                )
   225→        elif not filtered:
   226→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   227→
   228→    total = len(filtered)
   229→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   230→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   231→
   232→    if resolved_options.explain:
   233→        for item in filtered:
   234→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   235→
   236→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   237→    return {
   238→        "items": filtered,
   239→        "total": total,
   240→        "tier_counts": counts,
   241→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   242→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   243→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   244→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   245→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   246→    }
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = [
   250→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   251→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   252→    "WorkQueueResult",
   253→    "build_work_queue",
   254→    "group_queue_items",
   255→]
   256→
```

```
   134→    stale_since: str | None
   135→

   137→class ConcernDismissal(TypedDict, total=False):
   138→    """Record of a dismissed concern from review output."""
   139→
   140→    dismissed_at: str

   143→
   144→
   145→class StateModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   146→    version: Required[int]
   147→    created: Required[str]
   148→    last_scan: Required[str | None]
   149→    scan_count: Required[int]
   150→    overall_score: Required[float]
   151→    objective_score: Required[float]
   152→    strict_score: Required[float]
   153→    verified_strict_score: Required[float]
   154→    stats: Required[StateStats]
   155→    findings: Required[dict[str, Finding]]
   156→    scan_coverage: dict[str, ScanCoverageRecord]
   157→    score_confidence: dict[str, Any]
   158→    scan_history: list[ScanHistoryEntry]
   159→    subjective_integrity: Required[SubjectiveIntegrity]
   160→    subjective_assessments: Required[dict[str, SubjectiveAssessment]]

   168→    total_current: int

   170→    chronic_reopeners: list[dict]
   171→    skipped_other_lang: int
   172→    skipped_out_of_scope: int
   173→    ignored: int
   174→    ignore_patterns: int

   176→    suppressed_pct: float
   177→    skipped: NotRequired[int]
   178→    skipped_details: NotRequired[list[dict]]
   179→
   180→
   181→STATE_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
   182→STATE_FILE = STATE_DIR / "state.json"
   183→CURRENT_VERSION = 1
   184→
   185→
   186→def utc_now() -> str:
   187→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   188→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   189→
   190→
   191→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   192→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   193→    return {
   194→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   195→        "created": utc_now(),
   196→        "last_scan": None,
   197→        "scan_count": 0,
   198→        "overall_score": 0,
   199→        "objective_score": 0,
   200→        "strict_score": 0,
   201→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   202→        "stats": {},
   203→        "findings": {},
   204→        "scan_coverage": {},
   205→        "score_confidence": {},
   206→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   207→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   208→    }
   209→
   210→
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}
   228→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   229→        state["scan_history"] = []
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   231→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   233→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   235→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   236→
   237→    findings = state["findings"]
   238→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   239→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   240→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   241→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   245→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   246→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   247→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   248→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   249→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   250→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   251→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   252→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   253→            finding.get("status"),
   254→            default="open",
   255→        )
   256→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   257→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   258→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   259→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   260→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   261→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   262→        )
   263→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   264→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   265→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   266→
   267→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   268→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   269→
   270→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   271→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   272→            continue
   273→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   274→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   275→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   276→
   277→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   278→    return None
   279→
   280→
   281→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   282→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   283→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   284→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   285→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   286→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   287→
   288→    findings = state["findings"]
   289→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   290→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   291→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   292→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   293→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   294→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   295→            raise ValueError(
   296→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   297→            )
   298→
   299→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   300→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   301→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   302→
   303→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   304→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   305→            raise ValueError(
   306→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   307→            )
   308→
   309→
   310→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
   311→    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
   312→    if isinstance(obj, set):
   313→        return sorted(obj)
   314→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
   315→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
   316→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
   317→        return obj.isoformat()
   318→    raise TypeError(
   319→        f"Object of type {type(obj).__name__} is not JSON serializable: {obj!r}"
   320→    )
   321→
   322→
   323→def get_overall_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   324→    """Canonical overall score (lenient, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   325→    return state.get("overall_score")
   326→
   327→
   328→def get_objective_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   329→    """Canonical objective score (lenient, mechanical dimensions only)."""
   330→    return state.get("objective_score")
   331→
   332→
   333→def get_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   334→    """Canonical strict score (strict, includes subjective dimensions)."""
   335→    return state.get("strict_score")
   336→
   337→
   338→def get_verified_strict_score(state: StateModel) -> float | None:
   339→    """Strict score that only credits scan-verified fixes.
   340→
   341→    Returns None if no scan-verified score exists yet (fresh state or
   342→    no scan has run). Does not fall back to the unverified strict_score.
   343→    """
   344→    return state.get("verified_strict_score")
   345→
```

```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
     9→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    10→    "path_scoped_findings",
    11→    "is_ignored",
    12→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    13→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    14→    "add_ignore",
    15→    "make_finding",
    16→]
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    64→            continue
    65→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    66→            continue
    67→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    68→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    69→            in_scope += 1
    70→        else:
    71→            out_of_scope += 1
    72→
    73→    return {
    74→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    75→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    76→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    77→    }
    78→
    79→
    80→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    81→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    82→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    83→
    84→
    85→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    86→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    87→) -> str | None:
    88→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    89→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    90→        if "*" in pattern:
    91→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    92→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    93→                return pattern
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        if "::" in pattern:
    97→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
    98→                return pattern
    99→            continue
   100→
   101→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   102→            return pattern
   103→
   104→    return None
   105→
   106→
   107→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   108→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   109→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   110→    matched_ids = [
   111→        finding_id
   112→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   113→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   114→    ]
   115→    now = utc_now()
   116→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   117→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   118→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   119→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   120→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   121→        if finding.get("status") in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   122→            finding["status"] = "open"
   123→            finding["resolved_at"] = None
   124→            finding["note"] = (
   125→                "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   126→            )
   127→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   128→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   129→
   130→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   131→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   132→    return len(matched_ids)
   133→
   134→
   135→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   136→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   137→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   138→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   139→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   140→    if pattern not in ignores:
   141→        ignores.append(pattern)
   142→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   143→
   144→
   145→def make_finding(
   146→    detector: str,
   147→    file: str,
   148→    name: str,
   149→    *,
   150→    tier: int,
   151→    confidence: str,
   152→    summary: str,
   153→    detail: dict | None = None,
   154→) -> Finding:
   155→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   156→    rfile = rel(file)
   157→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   158→    now = utc_now()
   159→    return {
   160→        "id": finding_id,
   161→        "detector": detector,
   162→        "file": rfile,
   163→        "tier": tier,
   164→        "confidence": confidence,
   165→        "summary": summary,
   166→        "detail": detail or {},
   167→        "status": "open",
   168→        "note": None,
   169→        "first_seen": now,
   170→        "last_seen": now,
   171→        "resolved_at": None,
   172→        "reopen_count": 0,
   173→    }
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   178→    return (
   179→        finding_id == pattern
   180→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   181→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   182→        or (
   183→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   184→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   185→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   186→        )
   187→    )
   188→
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

```
     2→

     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult

    11→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    12→
    13→
    14→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    15→    state: dict,
    16→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    17→    *,
    18→    attestation: str | None,
    19→) -> list[str]:
    20→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    21→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    22→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    23→            state,
    24→            pattern,
    25→            args.status,
    26→            args.note,
    27→            attestation=attestation,
    28→        )
    29→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    30→    return all_resolved
    31→
    32→
    33→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    34→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    35→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    36→        payload,
    37→        context="resolve query payload update",
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    42→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    43→    write_query(
    44→        {
    45→            "command": "resolve",
    46→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    47→            "status": context.status,
    48→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    49→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    50→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    51→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    52→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    53→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    54→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    55→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    56→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    57→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    58→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    59→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    60→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    61→        }
    62→    )
    63→
```

```
   326→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   327→)
   328→
   329→_on_register_callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
   330→
   331→
   332→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   333→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   334→    _on_register_callbacks.append(callback)
   335→
   336→
   337→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   338→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   339→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   340→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   341→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   342→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   343→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = frozenset(
   344→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   345→    )
   346→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   347→        cb()
   348→
   349→
   350→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   351→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   352→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   353→
   354→
   355→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   356→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   357→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   358→
   359→
   360→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   361→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   362→    "auto_fix": "fix",
   363→    "reorganize": "move",
   364→    "refactor": "refactor",
   365→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   366→}
   367→
   368→
   369→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   370→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   371→    best = "manual"
   372→    best_pri = 99
   373→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   374→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   375→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   376→            if pri < best_pri:
   377→                best_pri = pri
   378→                best = d.action_type
   379→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   380→
   381→
   382→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   383→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   384→    result = {}
   385→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   386→        entry: dict = {
   387→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   388→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   389→        }
   390→        if d.tool:
   391→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   392→        if d.guidance:
   393→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   394→        result[name] = entry
   395→    return result
   396→
```

```
1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.
     2→
     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command
     4→(with subcommands) lives in ``desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd``.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import argparse
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    21→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    22→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    23→
    24→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    25→        return
    26→
    27→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    28→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    29→
    30→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    31→    if output:
    32→        try:
    33→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    34→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    35→            print_agent_plan(
    36→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    37→                next_command=next_command,
    38→            )
    39→        except OSError as e:
    40→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    41→    else:
    42→        print(plan_md)
    43→        print()
    44→        print_agent_plan(
    45→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    46→            next_command=next_command,
    47→        )
    48→
    49→
    50→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
```

```
     2→

    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   204→    return _build_merge_diff(
   205→        new_count=new_count,
   206→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   207→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   208→        current_ids=current_ids,
   209→        suspect_detectors=suspect_detectors,
   210→        chronic_reopeners=chronic_reopeners,
   211→        skipped_other_lang=skipped_other_lang,
   212→        skipped_out_of_scope=skipped_out_of_scope,
   213→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   214→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   215→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   216→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   217→    )
   218→
```

```
1→"""Finding upsert/auto-resolve helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     6→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import matches_exclusion
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open findings but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for finding in existing.values():
    21→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' findings enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always marked suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them.
    30→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    31→    suspect: set[str] = set()
    32→
    33→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    34→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    35→            suspect.add(detector)
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def auto_resolve_disappeared(
    50→    existing: dict,
    51→    current_ids: set[str],
    52→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    53→    now: str,
    54→    *,
    55→    lang: str | None,
    56→    scan_path: str | None,
    57→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    58→) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
    59→    """Auto-resolve open/wontfix/fixed/false_positive findings absent from scan.
    60→
    61→    Returns (resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors).
    62→    """
    63→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = skipped_out_of_scope = 0
    64→    resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
    65→
    66→    for finding_id, previous in existing.items():
    67→        if finding_id in current_ids or previous["status"] not in (
    68→            "open",
    69→            "wontfix",
    70→            "fixed",
    71→            "false_positive",
    72→        ):
    73→            continue
    74→
    75→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
    76→            skipped_other_lang += 1
    77→            continue
    78→
    79→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
    80→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    81→            if (
    82→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
    83→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
    84→            ):
    85→                skipped_out_of_scope += 1
    86→                continue
    87→
    88→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
    92→            continue
    93→
    94→        previous_status = previous["status"]
    95→        previous["status"] = "auto_resolved"
    96→        previous["resolved_at"] = now
    97→        previous["suppressed"] = False
    98→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
    99→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   100→        previous["resolution_attestation"] = {
   101→            "kind": "scan_verified",
   102→            "text": "Disappeared from detector output",
   103→            "attested_at": now,
   104→            "scan_verified": True,
   105→        }
   106→        previous["note"] = (
   107→            "Fixed despite wontfix — disappeared from scan (was wontfix)"
   108→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   109→            else "Disappeared from scan — likely fixed"
   110→        )
   111→        resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   112→        resolved += 1
   113→
   114→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors
   115→
   116→
   117→def upsert_findings(
   118→    existing: dict,
   119→    current_findings: list[dict],
   120→    ignore: list[str],
   121→    now: str,
   122→    *,
   123→    lang: str | None,
   124→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
   125→    """Insert new findings and update existing ones.
   126→
   127→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
   128→    """
   129→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   130→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   131→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   132→    changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
   133→
   134→    for finding in current_findings:
   135→        finding_id = finding["id"]
   136→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   137→        current_ids.add(finding_id)
   138→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   139→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)
   140→        if matched_ignore:
   141→            ignored_count += 1
   142→
   143→        if lang:
   144→            finding["lang"] = lang
   145→
   146→        if finding_id not in existing:
   147→            existing[finding_id] = dict(finding)
   148→            if matched_ignore:
   149→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed"] = True
   150→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   151→                existing[finding_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   152→                continue
   153→            new_count += 1
   154→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   155→            continue
   156→
   157→        previous = existing[finding_id]
   158→        previous.update(
   159→            last_seen=now,
   160→            tier=finding["tier"],
   161→            confidence=finding["confidence"],
   162→            summary=finding["summary"],
   163→            detail=finding.get("detail", {}),
   164→        )
   165→        if "zone" in finding:
   166→            previous["zone"] = finding["zone"]
   167→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   168→            previous["lang"] = lang
   169→
   170→        if matched_ignore:
   171→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   172→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   173→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   174→            if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   175→                previous["status"] = "open"
   176→                previous["resolved_at"] = None
   177→                previous["note"] = (
   178→                    "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   179→                )
   180→            continue
   181→
   182→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   183→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   184→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   185→
   186→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved"):
   187→            # subjective_review findings are condition-based.  When just
   188→            # auto-resolved by an agent import, skip reopening to avoid a
   189→            # resolve-then-reopen loop on the same scan cycle.
   190→            if (
   191→                detector == "subjective_review"
   192→                and previous["status"] == "auto_resolved"
   193→                and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
   194→            ):
   195→                continue
   196→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   197→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   198→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   199→            previous.update(
   200→                status="open",
   201→                resolved_at=None,
   202→                note=(
   203→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   204→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   205→                ),
   206→            )
   207→            reopened_count += 1
   208→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   209→
   210→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "auto_resolve_disappeared",
   215→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   216→    "upsert_findings",
   217→]
   218→
```
