1→"""Markdown plan rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from datetime import date
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import TIER_LABELS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanState
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS, DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.state import get_objective_score, get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    15→
    16→
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (
    24→        overall_score is not None
    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None
    27→    ):
    28→        header_score = (
    29→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→
    38→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def _plan_user_ordered_section(
   292→    items: list[dict],
   293→    plan: dict,
   294→) -> list[str]:
   295→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
   296→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   297→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   298→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   299→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   300→
   301→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
   302→    if not ordered_ids:
   303→        return []
   304→
   305→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   306→
   307→    lines: list[str] = [
   308→        "---",
   309→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
   310→        "",
   311→    ]
   312→
   313→    # Group by cluster: clustered items first, then unclustered
   314→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   315→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   316→        member_ids = [
   317→            fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   318→            if fid in ordered_ids and fid in by_id
   319→        ]
   320→        if not member_ids:
   321→            continue
   322→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   323→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   324→        if desc:
   325→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   326→        lines.append("")
   327→        for fid in member_ids:
   328→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   329→            if item:
   330→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   331→                emitted.add(fid)
   332→        lines.append("")
   333→
   334→    # Unclustered ordered items
   335→    unclustered = [
   336→        fid for fid in queue_order
   337→        if fid in ordered_ids and fid not in emitted and fid in by_id
   338→    ]
   339→    if unclustered:
   340→        if any(c.get("finding_ids") for c in clusters.values()):
   341→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   342→            lines.append("")
   343→        for fid in unclustered:
   344→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   345→            if item:
   346→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   347→        lines.append("")
   348→
   349→    return lines
   350→
   351→
   352→def _plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   353→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   354→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   355→    if not skipped:
   356→        return []
   357→
   358→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   359→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   360→
   361→    # Group by kind
   362→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   363→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   364→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   365→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(fid)
   366→
   367→    kind_labels = {
   368→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   369→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   370→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   371→    }
   372→
   373→    lines: list[str] = [
   374→        "---",
   375→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   376→        "",
   377→    ]
   378→
   379→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   380→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   381→        if not ids:
   382→            continue
   383→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   384→        lines.append("")
   385→        for fid in ids:
   386→            entry = skipped.get(fid, {})
   387→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   388→            if item:
   389→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   390→            else:
   391→                lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~ (not in current queue)")
   392→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   393→            if reason:
   394→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   395→            note = entry.get("note")
   396→            if note and not overrides.get(fid, {}).get("note"):
   397→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   398→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   399→            if review_after:
   400→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   401→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   402→        lines.append("")
   403→
   404→    return lines
   405→
   406→
   407→def _plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   408→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   409→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   410→    if not superseded:
   411→        return []
   412→
   413→    lines: list[str] = [
   414→        "---",
   415→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   416→        "",
   417→    ]
   418→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   419→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   420→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   421→        lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~{summary_str}")
   422→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   423→        if candidates:
   424→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   425→        note = entry.get("note")
   426→        if note:
   427→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   428→    lines.append("")
   429→    return lines
   430→
   431→
   432→def _render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
   433→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
   434→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   435→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   436→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   437→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
   438→
   439→    lines = [f"- [ ] [T{tier}/{confidence}] {summary}"]
   440→    desc = override.get("description")
   441→    if desc:
   442→        lines.append(f"      → {desc}")
   443→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
   444→    note = override.get("note")
   445→    if note:
   446→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   447→    return lines
   448→
   449→
   450→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   451→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   452→
   453→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   454→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   455→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   456→    """
   457→    findings = state["findings"]
   458→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   459→
   460→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   461→    if plan is None:
   462→        try:
   463→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   464→            plan = load_plan()
   465→        except Exception:
   466→            plan = {}
   467→
   468→    has_plan = bool(
   469→        plan
   470→        and (
   471→            plan.get("queue_order")
   472→            or plan.get("skipped")
   473→            or plan.get("clusters")
   474→        )
   475→    )
   476→
   477→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   478→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   479→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   480→
   481→    if has_plan:
   482→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   483→        queue = build_work_queue(
   484→            state,
   485→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   486→                count=None,
   487→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   488→                status="open",
   489→                include_subjective=True,
   490→                no_tier_fallback=True,
   491→            ),
   492→        )
   493→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   494→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   495→
   496→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   497→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   498→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   499→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   500→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   501→        if remaining:
   502→            lines.append("---")
   503→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   504→            lines.append("")
   505→
   506→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   507→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   508→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   509→    else:
   510→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   511→
   512→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   513→
   514→    return "\n".join(lines)
   515→
```

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "PLAN_FILE",
   107→    "has_living_plan",
   108→    "load_plan",
   109→    "plan_path_for_state",
   110→    "save_plan",
   111→]
   112→

1→"""Plan cluster subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    add_to_cluster,
    13→    create_cluster,
    14→    delete_cluster,
    15→    load_plan,

    21→
    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    plan = load_plan()
    26→    try:
    27→        create_cluster(plan, name, description)
    28→    except ValueError as ex:
    29→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    30→        return
    31→    save_plan(plan)
    32→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    36→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    37→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    38→        return
    39→
    40→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    41→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    42→
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    45→    if not finding_ids:
    46→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    47→        return
    48→    try:
    49→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    50→    except ValueError as ex:
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    52→        return
    53→    save_plan(plan)
    54→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    59→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    63→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    64→
    65→    plan = load_plan()
    66→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    67→    if not finding_ids:
    68→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    69→        return
    70→    try:
    71→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    72→    except ValueError as ex:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    74→        return
    75→    save_plan(plan)
    76→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    77→
    78→
    79→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    80→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    81→    plan = load_plan()
    82→    try:
    83→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
    84→    except ValueError as ex:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    86→        return
    87→    save_plan(plan)
    88→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
    89→
    90→
    91→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    92→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    93→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    94→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    95→
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→
    98→    offset: int | None = None
    99→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   100→        try:
   101→            offset = int(target)
   102→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   103→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   104→            return
   105→        target = None
   106→
   107→    try:
   108→        count = move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   109→    except ValueError as ex:
   110→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   111→        return
   112→    save_plan(plan)
   113→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   114→
   115→
   116→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   117→    plan = load_plan()
   118→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→    if not clusters:
   121→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   122→        return
   123→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   124→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   125→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   127→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   128→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   129→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   130→
   131→
   132→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   133→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   134→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   135→    dispatch = {
   136→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   137→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   138→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   139→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   140→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   141→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   142→    }
   143→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   144→    if handler is None:
   145→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   146→        return
   147→    handler(args)
   148→
   149→
   150→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   151→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:

   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))

   128→            "tier asc",

   131→            "count desc",

   133→        ]
   134→    else:

   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        elif group == "cluster":
   182→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   183→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   184→        else:
   185→            key = "items"
   186→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   187→    return grouped
   188→

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    original_id: Required[str]
    44→    original_detector: str
    45→    original_file: str
    46→    original_summary: str
    47→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    48→    superseded_at: str
    49→    remapped_to: str | None
    50→    candidates: list[str]
    51→    note: str | None
    52→
    53→
    54→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    55→    version: Required[int]
    56→    created: Required[str]
    57→    updated: Required[str]
    58→    queue_order: list[str]
    59→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    60→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    61→    active_cluster: str | None
    62→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    63→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    64→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    65→
    66→
    67→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    68→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    69→    now = utc_now()
    70→    return {
    71→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    72→        "created": now,
    73→        "updated": now,
    74→        "queue_order": [],
    75→        "deferred": [],
    76→        "skipped": {},
    77→        "active_cluster": None,
    78→        "overrides": {},
    79→        "clusters": {},
    80→        "superseded": {},
    81→    }
    82→
    83→
    84→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    85→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    86→
    87→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    88→    """
    89→    defaults = empty_plan()
    90→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    91→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    92→
    93→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    94→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    95→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
    96→        plan["deferred"] = []
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
    98→        plan["skipped"] = {}
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   100→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   102→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   104→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   105→
   106→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   107→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   108→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   109→    if deferred:
   110→        now = utc_now()
   111→        for fid in list(deferred):
   112→            if fid not in skipped:
   113→                skipped[fid] = {
   114→                    "finding_id": fid,
   115→                    "kind": "temporary",
   116→                    "reason": None,
   117→                    "note": None,
   118→                    "attestation": None,
   119→                    "created_at": now,
   120→                    "review_after": None,
   121→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   122→                }
   123→        deferred.clear()
   124→
   125→    # Normalize cluster finding_ids
   126→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   127→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   128→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   129→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   130→
   131→
   132→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   133→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   134→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   135→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   136→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   137→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   138→
   139→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   140→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   141→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   142→    if overlap:
   143→        raise ValueError(
   144→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   145→        )
   146→
   147→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   148→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   149→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   150→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   151→            raise ValueError(
   152→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   153→            )
   154→
   155→
   156→__all__ = [
   157→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   158→    "Cluster",
   159→    "ItemOverride",
   160→    "PlanModel",
   161→    "SkipEntry",
   162→    "SupersededEntry",
   163→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   164→    "empty_plan",
   165→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   166→    "validate_plan",
   167→]
   168→
```

    71→    if capabilities is None:

    73→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
    74→        return normalized

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel, name: str, description: str | None = None
   248→) -> Cluster:
   249→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   250→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   251→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   252→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   253→    now = utc_now()
   254→    cluster: Cluster = {
   255→        "name": name,
   256→        "description": description,
   257→        "finding_ids": [],
   258→        "created_at": now,
   259→        "updated_at": now,
   260→    }
   261→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   262→    return cluster
   263→
   264→
   265→def add_to_cluster(
   266→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   267→) -> int:
   268→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   269→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   270→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   271→    if cluster is None:
   272→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   273→
   274→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   275→    count = 0
   276→    now = utc_now()
   277→    for fid in finding_ids:
   278→        if fid not in member_ids:
   279→            member_ids.append(fid)
   280→            count += 1
   281→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   282→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   283→        if fid not in overrides:
   284→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   285→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   286→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   287→
   288→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   289→    return count
   290→
   291→
   292→def remove_from_cluster(
   293→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   294→) -> int:
   295→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   296→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   297→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   298→    if cluster is None:
   299→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   300→
   301→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   302→    now = utc_now()
   303→    count = 0
   304→    for fid in finding_ids:
   305→        if fid in member_ids:
   306→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   307→            count += 1
   308→        # Clear cluster from override
   309→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   310→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   311→            override["cluster"] = None
   312→            override["updated_at"] = now
   313→
   314→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   315→    return count
   316→
   317→
   318→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   319→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   320→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   321→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   322→    if cluster is None:
   323→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   324→
   325→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   326→    now = utc_now()
   327→    for fid in orphaned:
   328→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   329→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   330→            override["cluster"] = None
   331→            override["updated_at"] = now
   332→
   333→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   334→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   335→
   336→    return orphaned
   337→
   338→
   339→def move_cluster(
   340→    plan: PlanModel,
   341→    cluster_name: str,
   342→    position: str,
   343→    target: str | None = None,
   344→    offset: int | None = None,
   345→) -> int:
   346→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   347→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   348→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   349→    if cluster is None:
   350→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   351→
   352→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   353→    if not member_ids:
   354→        return 0
   355→
   356→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   357→
   358→
   359→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   360→# Focus
   361→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   362→
   363→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   364→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   365→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   366→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   367→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   368→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   369→
   370→
   371→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   372→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   373→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   374→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   375→
   376→
   377→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   378→# Reset
   379→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   380→
   381→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   382→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   383→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   384→    plan.clear()
   385→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   386→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   387→        plan[k] = v
   388→    plan["created"] = created
   389→
   390→
   391→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   392→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   393→
   394→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   395→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   396→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   397→    """
   398→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   399→    found = 0
   400→
   401→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   402→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   403→    for fid in finding_ids:
   404→        was_present = False
   405→        if fid in order:
   406→            order.remove(fid)
   407→            was_present = True
   408→        if fid in skipped:
   409→            skipped.pop(fid)
   410→            was_present = True
   411→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   412→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   413→            if fid in ids:
   414→                ids.remove(fid)
   415→                was_present = True
   416→        if was_present:
   417→            found += 1
   418→
   419→    return found
   420→
   421→
   422→__all__ = [
   423→    "add_to_cluster",
   424→    "annotate_finding",
   425→    "clear_focus",
   426→    "create_cluster",
   427→    "delete_cluster",
   428→    "describe_finding",
   429→    "move_cluster",
   430→    "move_items",
   431→    "purge_ids",
   432→    "remove_from_cluster",
   433→    "reset_plan",
   434→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   435→    "set_focus",
   436→    "skip_items",
   437→    "unskip_items",
   438→]
   439→

Perfect! Now I have everything I need. Let me provide you with a comprehensive analysis of the work queue architecture and data model.

## Complete Work Queue Architecture & Data Model

### 1. **ITEM DICT — Complete Field Inventory**

An "item" is a plain Python `dict` with the following fields:

#### Core Finding Fields (inherited from state)
- `id`: str — unique finding/item identifier (e.g., `"detector::hash"` or `"subjective::slug"`)
- `kind`: str — `"finding"` or `"subjective_dimension"` (defaults to `"finding"`)
- `detector`: str — detector name (e.g., `"review"`, `"dead_code"`, `"subjective_assessment"`, `"holistic_review"`)
- `file`: str — file path or `"."` for project-level findings
- `tier`: int — original tier (1–4, default 3)
- `effective_tier`: int — computed tier (always 4 for subjective; mechanical uses original tier)
- `confidence`: str — one of `"high"`, `"medium"`, `"low"` (default `"medium"`)
- `summary`: str — human-readable one-line description
- `status`: str — one of `"open"`, `"fixed"`, `"wontfix"`, `"suppressed"`
- `detail`: dict — detector-specific metadata (count, lines, category, suggestion, dimension, etc.)

#### Computed Fields Added by Queue Building
- `is_review`: bool — True if `detector == "review"`
- `is_subjective`: bool — True if detector is `"subjective_assessment"` or `"holistic_review"`
- `review_weight`: float | None — ranking multiplier for review findings (high=1.0, medium=0.7, low=0.3; holistic ×10)
- `subjective_score`: float | None — dimension's strict score (only for subjective items; queried from `state.dimension_scores`)
- `primary_command`: str — recommended CLI command to address the finding
- `supported_fixers`: not stored in item; used during build phase

#### Ranking Fields
- `explain`: dict (optional) — ranking rationale when `--explain` flag is used
  - Fields: `kind`, `effective_tier`, `confidence`, `confidence_rank`, `count`, `id`, `ranking_factors`, (optional: `review_weight`, `subjective_score`, `policy`)

#### Plan Overlay Fields (added by `_apply_plan_order()`)
- `queue_position`: int — position in reordered queue (1-indexed)
- `plan_description`: str (optional) — override description from plan
- `plan_note`: str (optional) — contextual note from plan
- `plan_cluster`: dict (optional) — cluster metadata:
  - `name`: str
  - `description`: str | None
  - `total_items`: int — total items in cluster
  - `sibling_ids`: list[str] — all finding IDs in this cluster
- `plan_skipped`: bool (optional) — True if item is deferred in plan
- `plan_skip_kind`: str — `"temporary"` or `"permanent"`
- `plan_skip_reason`: str (optional) — user's reason for deferring

### 2. **ITEM SORT KEY — Ranking Algorithm**

Function: `item_sort_key(item) -> tuple` in `ranking.py`

**For Subjective Items** (returned as a 4-tuple):
```python
(
    effective_tier,        # 4 (always)
    1,                     # Subjective sorts after mechanical within T4
    subjective_score,      # Lower score = higher priority (ascending)
    id,                    # Tie-breaker
)
```

**For Mechanical Items** (returned as a 6-tuple):
```python
(
    effective_tier,        # T1, T2, T3 (ascending = higher priority)
    0,                     # Mechanical sorts before subjective within tier
    confidence_rank,       # CONFIDENCE_ORDER["high"]=0, "medium"=1, "low"=2
    -review_weight,        # Negated (higher weight = higher priority)
    -count,                # Negated (more issues = higher priority)
    id,                    # Tie-breaker
)
```

**Confidence ranking** (from `planning/common.py`):
```python
CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
```

**Result**: Items sorted by tier asc → mechanical before subjective → confidence asc → review weight desc → count desc → id asc.

### 3. **BUILD_WORK_QUEUE() — Full Orchestration**

Function: `build_work_queue(state, options=None) -> WorkQueueResult` in `core.py`

**Inputs**:
- `state`: StateModel dict with keys like `findings`, `dimension_scores`, `scan_path`, `lang_capabilities`
- `options`: QueueBuildOptions dataclass

**QueueBuildOptions fields**:
```python
tier: int | None = None           # Filter to specific tier
count: int | None = 1             # Return top N items
scan_path: str | None = None      # Scan scope (for path filtering)
scope: str | None = None          # Pattern filter (ID, detector, file, dimension, summary)
status: str = "open"              # Filter status ("open", "fixed", "wontfix", "all")
include_subjective: bool = True   # Add synthetic subjective dimension items
subjective_threshold: float = 100.0  # Only include subjective dims below this %
chronic: bool = False             # Only reopen_count >= 2 items
no_tier_fallback: bool = False    # Don't fallback if requested tier empty
explain: bool = False             # Include ranking explanation per item
plan: dict | None = None          # Living plan dict
include_skipped: bool = False     # Include deferred items in output
cluster: str | None = None        # Filter to specific cluster by name
```

**Process**:
1. **Build finding items**: `build_finding_items()` → list of dicts from `state.findings`
   - Filters: suppressed, status, scan_path, scope, chronic, tier assignment, scoring
   - Adds: `id`, `kind="finding"`, `is_review`, `is_subjective`, `effective_tier`, `review_weight`, `subjective_score`, `primary_command`

2. **Build subjective items** (if enabled): `build_subjective_items()` → synthetic tier-4 items
   - One per scorecard dimension below `subjective_threshold`
   - Kind: `"subjective_dimension"`
   - Detector: `"subjective_assessment"`
   - File: `"."`

3. **Merge and sort**: Combine all items, sort by `item_sort_key()`, count tiers

4. **Apply plan overlay** (if plan provided): `_apply_plan_order()` reorders + annotates
   - Reorders by `queue_order` list
   - Adds plan metadata (description, cluster, skip flags)
   - Filters to cluster if `cluster` arg provided

5. **Tier filtering**:
   - If `tier` specified: filter to that tier
   - If empty and fallback enabled: choose nearest non-empty tier, set fallback reason
   - If empty and no fallback: keep empty, set fallback reason

6. **Count limit**: Slice to `count` items

7. **Explain** (if requested): Call `item_explain(item)` for each, adds `explain` field

**Returns WorkQueueResult**:
```python
{
    "items": list[dict],              # Filtered/limited items
    "total": int,                     # Count of items matching tier filter (pre-limit)
    "tier_counts": {1: n, 2: n, ...}, # Total counts for all tiers
    "requested_tier": int | None,
    "selected_tier": int | None,      # May differ if fallback applied
    "fallback_reason": str | None,    # "Requested T3 has 0 open → showing T2..."
    "available_tiers": [1, 2, 3, 4],  # Tiers with > 0 items
    "grouped": dict[str, list],       # Items grouped by "item" (not used currently)
}
```

### 4. **_APPLY_PLAN_ORDER() — Plan Overlay Details**

Function: `_apply_plan_order(items, plan, include_skipped=False, cluster=None) -> list[dict]`

**Plan dict structure**:
```python
{
    "queue_order": ["id1", "id2", ...],       # Explicitly ordered item IDs
    "skipped": {                              # Deferred items
        "id": {"kind": "temporary", "reason": "..."}
    },
    "overrides": {                            # Per-item customization
        "id": {"description": "...", "note": "...", "cluster": "name"}
    },
    "clusters": {                             # Item grouping
        "cluster_name": {
            "description": "...",
            "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2", ...]
        }
    },
    "active_cluster": "cluster_name"          # Focus on this cluster
}
```

**Steps**:
1. Build by-ID lookup of all items
2. For each override, annotate item with `plan_description`, `plan_note`, `plan_cluster`
3. Reorder: `queue_order` items first → remaining → skipped (if included)
4. Assign `queue_position` (1-indexed) to each item
5. If `cluster` arg or `active_cluster`: filter to cluster members only
6. Return reordered list

### 5. **SERIALIZE_ITEM() — Query.json Output**

Function: `serialize_item(item) -> dict` in `output.py`

Output dict fields:
```python
{
    "id": str,
    "kind": str,
    "tier": int,
    "effective_tier": int,
    "confidence": str,
    "detector": str,
    "file": str,
    "summary": str,
    "detail": dict,
    "status": str,
    "primary_command": str,
    
    # Optional conditional fields:
    "explain": dict,                # If item["explain"] exists
    "queue_position": int,          # If item["queue_position"] exists
    "plan_description": str,        # If item["plan_description"] exists
    "plan_note": str,               # If item["plan_note"] exists
    "plan_cluster": dict,           # If item["plan_cluster"] exists
    "plan_skipped": bool,           # If item["plan_skipped"] exists
    "plan_skip_kind": str,
    "plan_skip_reason": str,        # If exists
}
```

**build_query_payload()** wraps serialized items into:
```python
{
    "command": "next",
    "items": [serialized_item, ...],
    "queue": {
        "tier_counts": {...},
        "requested_tier": int | None,
        "selected_tier": int | None,
        "fallback_reason": str | None,
        "available_tiers": [int, ...],
        "total": int,
    },
    "narrative": {...},
    
    # Optional if plan active:
    "plan": {
        "active": bool,
        "focus": str | None,
        "clusters": [{name, description, item_count}, ...],
        "total_ordered": int,
        "total_skipped": int,
        "plan_overrides_narrative": bool,
    },
    
    # If invoked from next command:
    "overall_score": float | None,
    "objective_score": float | None,
    "strict_score": float | None,
    "scorecard_dimensions": [...],
    "subjective_measures": [...],
}
```

### 6. **RENDER TERMINAL — Single Item Render Function**

Function: `_render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain, potentials=None) -> None` in `render.py` (lines 92–275)

**Renders**:
1. **Header**: Tier, confidence
2. **Type label**: "Design review (requires judgment)", "Auto-fixable", or none
3. **Subjective-specific** (if kind == "subjective_dimension"):
   - Dimension name
   - Score percentage
   - Primary command hint
   - Re-review behavior note
4. **Plan overrides** (if present):
   - Description override
   - Cluster membership (name, description, total items)
   - User note
5. **File & ID** (with dimmed color)
6. **Detail fields**: Lines, category, importers, suggestion
7. **Code snippet**: Reading from file if available
8. **Dimension health**: Score, percentage, checks failing
9. **Score impact estimate**: Fixing this item worth ~X points
10. **Similar findings**: If auto-fixable detector with >1 similar open
11. **Explain block** (if `--explain`): Ranking policy, factors, dimension context

**Iteration**:
- `render_terminal_items()` in `next.py` loops over items
- For multiple items: shows `[#1/N]`, `[#2/N]`, etc., or plan `queue_position` if available
- Calls `_render_item()` per item, separated by blank line

### 7. **GROUPING LOGIC**

Function: `group_queue_items(items, group_by) -> dict[str, list[dict]]` in `ranking.py`

**Grouping modes**:
- `"item"` → `{"items": [all items]}` (default, no real grouping)
- `"file"` → `{filepath: [items from that file], ...}`
- `"detector"` → `{detector_name: [items with that detector], ...}`
- `"tier"` → `{"T1": [...], "T2": [...], "T3": [...], "T4": [...]}`
- `"cluster"` → `{cluster_name: [items in cluster], "(unclustered)": [items without cluster]}`

When group != `"item"`, `_render_grouped()` renders as:
```
Grouping Key (N items)
  T1 [medium] [auto] Item summary
  T1 [high] Item summary
  ...
```

**Current usage**: Always called with `group="item"` in queue result (line 245 of core.py), but CLI supports `--group file|detector|tier|cluster`.

### 8. **HOW NEXT --COUNT WORKS**

- `next --count 1` → top 1 item
- `next --count 10` → top 10 items
- Applied **after** tier filtering and plan reordering (line 229–230 of core.py: `filtered[: resolved_options.count]`)
- If no `--count`: defaults to 1 (QueueBuildOptions.count default)

### 9. **EXISTING AUTO-CLUSTERING FEATURE**

The living plan **already has basic clustering**:
- `plan["clusters"][cluster_name]["finding_ids"]` — manually curated groups
- `plan["active_cluster"]` — focus on one cluster
- `--cluster arg` — filter queue to cluster
- Serialized in query.json with item count per cluster

**Limitations**:
- Manual curation (no auto-grouping algorithm)
- Filtering is binary (show only cluster members or all)
- No "related findings" suggestion or automatic grouping by detector/file/dimension
- No batching concept (items shown one-by-one sequentially)

### 10. **DESIGN SURFACE FOR AUTO-CLUSTERING**

For auto-clustering, consider:
1. **Grouping key**: Add optional `cluster_key` field to item dict (computed during build)
2. **Suggestion algorithm**: In helpers.py, add function like `compute_natural_clusters(items) -> dict[str, list[str]]`
3. **Presentation**:
   - Render cluster header + item count before first member
   - Show sibling count in item details
   - Optional `--group cluster` in next command (already supported)
4. **Query.json**: Include suggested clusters in `payload["auto_clusters"]` or similar
5. **Plan integration**: Auto-clusters can be promoted to manual clusters via `plan cluster move`

---

**Summary**: Items are plain dicts with 20+ fields (core + computed + plan overlay). Ranking is deterministic 6-tuple sort. Plan reorders and annotates. Serialization is selective (optional fields). Rendering iterates per item with detailed contextual output. Auto-clustering can hook into the build phase or post-process the ranked queue.
