```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
```

```
1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    24→
    25→
    26→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    27→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    28→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    29→    if not plan_path.exists():
    30→        return empty_plan()
    31→
    32→    try:
    33→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    34→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    35→        print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    36→        return empty_plan()
    37→
    38→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    39→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    40→        return empty_plan()
    41→
    42→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    43→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    44→        print(
    45→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    46→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    47→            file=sys.stderr,
    48→        )
    49→
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    51→    try:
    52→        validate_plan(data)
    53→    except ValueError as ex:
    54→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    55→        return empty_plan()
    56→
    57→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    58→
    59→
    60→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    61→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    62→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    63→
    64→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    65→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    66→    validate_plan(plan)
    67→
    68→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    69→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    70→
    71→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    72→
    73→    if plan_path.exists():
    74→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    75→        try:
    76→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    77→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    78→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    82→    except OSError as ex:
    83→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
    84→        raise
    85→
    86→
    87→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    88→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    89→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    90→
    91→
    92→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
    93→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
    94→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    95→    if not plan_path.exists():
    96→        return False
    97→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    98→    return bool(
    99→        plan.get("queue_order")
   100→        or plan.get("overrides")
   101→        or plan.get("clusters")
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→__all__ = [
   106→    "PLAN_FILE",
   107→    "has_living_plan",
   108→    "load_plan",
   109→    "plan_path_for_state",
   110→    "save_plan",
   111→]
   112→
```
```

1→"""Sync stale subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Handles both directions:
     4→  1. **Cleanup** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale.
     5→  2. **Inject**  — when the queue is empty after cleanup, add stale dimensions.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→
    15→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    20→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    21→
    22→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→
    25→    @property
    26→    def changes(self) -> int:
    27→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    32→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    33→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    34→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    38→    if not dim_scores:
    39→        return set()
    40→
    41→    stale: set[str] = set()
    42→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    43→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    44→            continue
    45→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    46→        if dim_key:
    47→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    48→    return stale
    49→
    50→
    51→def sync_stale_dimensions(
    52→    plan: PlanModel,
    53→    state: StateModel,
    54→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    55→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
    56→
    57→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
    58→       longer stale (the user refreshed them, or mechanical evidence changed).
    59→    2. If the queue is empty after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
    60→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    61→    """
    62→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    63→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    64→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    65→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    66→
    67→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    68→    to_remove: list[str] = [
    69→        fid for fid in order
    70→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in stale_ids
    71→    ]
    72→    for fid in to_remove:
    73→        order.remove(fid)
    74→        result.pruned.append(fid)
    75→
    76→    # --- Inject: populate when queue is empty ----------------------------
    77→    if not order and stale_ids:
    78→        existing = set(order)
    79→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
    80→            if sid not in existing:
    81→                order.append(sid)
    82→                result.injected.append(sid)
    83→
    84→    return result
    85→
    86→
    87→__all__ = [
    88→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    89→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    90→]
    91→
```

```
   322→
   323→_DISPLAY_ORDER = list(DISPLAY_ORDER)
   324→
   325→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   326→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   327→)
   328→
   329→_on_register_callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
   330→
   331→
   332→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   333→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   334→    _on_register_callbacks.append(callback)
   335→
   336→
   337→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   338→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   339→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   340→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   341→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   342→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   343→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = frozenset(
   344→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   345→    )
   346→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   347→        cb()
   348→
   349→
   350→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   351→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   352→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   353→
   354→
   355→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   356→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   357→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   358→
   359→
   360→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   361→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   362→    "auto_fix": "fix",
   363→    "reorganize": "move",
   364→    "refactor": "refactor",
   365→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   366→}
   367→
   368→
   369→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   370→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   371→    best = "manual"
   372→    best_pri = 99
   373→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   374→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   375→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   376→            if pri < best_pri:
   377→                best_pri = pri
   378→                best = d.action_type
   379→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   380→
   381→
   382→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   383→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   384→    result = {}
   385→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   386→        entry: dict = {
   387→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   388→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   389→        }
   390→        if d.tool:
   391→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   392→        if d.guidance:
   393→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   394→        result[name] = entry
   395→    return result
   396→
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
```

```
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    89→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    90→
    91→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    92→
    93→    # Load the living plan
    94→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    95→    plan = load_plan()
    96→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    97→    if (
    98→        plan.get("queue_order")
    99→        or plan.get("overrides")
   100→        or plan.get("clusters")
   101→    ):
   102→        plan_data = plan
   103→
   104→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   105→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   106→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   107→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   108→        if active_cluster:
   109→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   110→
   111→    queue = build_work_queue(
   112→        state,
   113→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   114→            tier=tier,
   115→            count=count,
   116→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   117→            scope=scope,
   118→            status=status,
   119→            include_subjective=True,
   120→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   121→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   122→            explain=explain,
   123→            plan=plan_data,
   124→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   125→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   126→        ),
   127→    )
   128→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   129→
   130→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   131→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   132→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   133→        state,
   134→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   138→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   139→    )
   140→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   141→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   142→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   143→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   144→        state,
   145→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   146→    )
   147→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   148→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   149→    ]
   150→    write_query(payload)
   151→
   152→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   153→    if output_file:
   154→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   155→            output_file,
   156→            payload,
   157→            len(items),
   158→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   159→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   160→        ):
   161→            return
   162→        raise SystemExit(1)
   163→
   164→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   165→        return
   166→
   167→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   168→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   169→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   170→        return
   171→
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   173→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   174→        state.get("findings", {}),
   175→        state.get("scan_path"),
   176→    )
   177→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   178→    try:
   179→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   180→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   181→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   182→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   183→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   184→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   185→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   186→    )
   187→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   188→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   189→        state,
   190→        dim_scores,
   191→        findings_scoped,
   192→        strict_score=strict_score,
   193→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   194→    )
   195→    print()
   196→
   197→
   198→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   199→
```

```
    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    plan = load_plan()
    26→    try:
    27→        create_cluster(plan, name, description)
    28→    except ValueError as ex:
    29→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    30→        return
    31→    save_plan(plan)
    32→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    36→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    37→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    38→        return
    39→
    40→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    41→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    42→
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    45→    if not finding_ids:
    46→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    47→        return
    48→    try:
    49→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    50→    except ValueError as ex:
    51→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    52→        return
    53→    save_plan(plan)
    54→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    58→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    59→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    63→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    64→
    65→    plan = load_plan()
    66→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    67→    if not finding_ids:
    68→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    69→        return
    70→    try:
    71→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    72→    except ValueError as ex:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    74→        return
    75→    save_plan(plan)
    76→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    77→
    78→
    79→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    80→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    81→    plan = load_plan()
    82→    try:
    83→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
    84→    except ValueError as ex:
    85→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    86→        return
    87→    save_plan(plan)
    88→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
    89→
    90→
    91→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    92→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    93→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    94→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    95→
    96→    plan = load_plan()
    97→
    98→    offset: int | None = None
    99→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   100→        try:
   101→            offset = int(target)
   102→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   103→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   104→            return
   105→        target = None
   106→
   107→    try:
   108→        count = move_cluster(plan, cluster_name, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   109→    except ValueError as ex:
   110→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   111→        return
   112→    save_plan(plan)
   113→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster {cluster_name} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   114→
   115→
   116→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   117→    plan = load_plan()
   118→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→    if not clusters:
   121→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   122→        return
   123→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   124→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   125→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   127→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   128→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   129→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   130→
   131→
   132→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   133→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   134→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   135→    dispatch = {
   136→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   137→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   138→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   139→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   140→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   141→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   142→    }
   143→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   144→    if handler is None:
   145→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   146→        return
   147→    handler(args)
   148→
   149→
   150→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
```

```
1→"""Pattern → finding-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import match_findings
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→
     9→
    10→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    11→    state: StateModel,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    15→    status_filter: str = "open",
    16→) -> list[str]:
    17→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of finding IDs.
    18→
    19→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    20→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    21→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["findings"]``.
    22→    """
    23→    seen: set[str] = set()
    24→    result: list[str] = []
    25→
    26→    # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
    27→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    28→    if plan is not None:
    29→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    30→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    31→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    32→            plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    33→
    34→    for pattern in patterns:
    35→        matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    36→        if matches:
    37→            for finding in matches:
    38→                fid = finding["id"]
    39→                if fid not in seen:
    40→                    seen.add(fid)
    41→                    result.append(fid)
    42→        elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
    43→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state findings
    44→            seen.add(pattern)
    45→            result.append(pattern)
    46→    return result
    47→
    48→
    49→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    50→
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    15→        "id": item.get("id"),
    16→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    17→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    18→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    19→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": item.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    24→        "status": item.get("status"),
    25→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→    }
    27→    explain = item.get("explain")
    28→    if explain is not None:
    29→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    30→
    31→    # Plan metadata
    32→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    33→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    34→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    35→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    36→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    37→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    38→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    39→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    40→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    41→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    42→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    43→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    44→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    45→
    46→    return serialized
    47→
    48→
    49→def build_query_payload(
    50→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    51→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    52→    *,
    53→    command: str,
    54→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    55→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    56→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    57→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    58→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    59→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    60→        "command": command,
    61→        "items": serialized,
    62→        "queue": {
    63→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    64→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    65→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    66→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    67→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    68→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    69→        },
    70→        "narrative": narrative,
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    if plan and (
    74→        plan.get("queue_order")
    75→        or plan.get("skipped")
    76→        or plan.get("clusters")
    77→    ):
    78→        clusters_summary = []
    79→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    80→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    81→            clusters_summary.append({
    82→                "name": name,
    83→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
    84→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
    85→            })
    86→        payload["plan"] = {
    87→            "active": True,
    88→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
    89→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
    90→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
    91→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
    92→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
    93→        }
    94→
    95→    return payload
    96→
    97→
    98→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
    99→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   100→    lines = [
   101→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   102→        "",
   103→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   104→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   105→    ]
   106→    for item in items:
   107→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   108→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   109→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   110→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   111→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   112→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   113→    lines.append("")
   114→    return "\n".join(lines)
   115→
   116→
   117→def write_output_file(
   118→    output_file: str,
   119→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   120→    item_count: int,
   121→    *,
   122→    safe_write_text_fn,
   123→    colorize_fn,
   124→) -> bool:
   125→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   126→    try:
   127→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   128→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   129→    except OSError as exc:
   130→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   131→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   132→        return False
   133→    return True
   134→
   135→
   136→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   137→    output_format: str,
   138→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   139→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   140→) -> bool:
   141→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   142→    renderers = {
   143→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   144→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   145→    }
   146→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   147→    if renderer is None:
   148→        return False
   149→    renderer()
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→__all__ = [
   154→    "build_query_payload",
   155→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   156→    "render_markdown",
   157→    "serialize_item",
   158→    "write_output_file",
   159→]
   160→
```

```
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",

    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
```

```
60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    64→            continue
    65→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    66→            continue
    67→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    68→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    69→            in_scope += 1
    70→        else:
    71→            out_of_scope += 1
    72→
    73→    return {
    74→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    75→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    76→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    77→    }
    78→
    79→
    80→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    81→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    82→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    83→
    84→
    85→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    86→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    87→) -> str | None:
    88→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    89→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    90→        if "*" in pattern:
    91→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    92→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    93→                return pattern
    94→            continue
    95→
    96→        if "::" in pattern:
    97→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
    98→                return pattern
    99→            continue
   100→
   101→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   102→            return pattern
   103→
   104→    return None
   105→
   106→
   107→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   108→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   109→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   110→    matched_ids = [
   111→        finding_id
   112→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   113→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   114→    ]
   115→    now = utc_now()
   116→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   117→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   118→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   119→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   120→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   121→        if finding.get("status") in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   122→            finding["status"] = "open"
   123→            finding["resolved_at"] = None
   124→            finding["note"] = (
   125→                "Suppressed by ignore pattern — remains unresolved for score integrity"
   126→            )
   127→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   128→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   129→
   130→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   131→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   132→    return len(matched_ids)
   133→
   134→
   135→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   136→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   137→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   138→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   139→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   140→    if pattern not in ignores:
   141→        ignores.append(pattern)
   142→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   143→
   144→
   145→def make_finding(
   146→    detector: str,
   147→    file: str,
   148→    name: str,
   149→    *,
   150→    tier: int,
   151→    confidence: str,
   152→    summary: str,
   153→    detail: dict | None = None,
   154→) -> Finding:
   155→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   156→    rfile = rel(file)
   157→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   158→    now = utc_now()
   159→    return {
   160→        "id": finding_id,
   161→        "detector": detector,
   162→        "file": rfile,
   163→        "tier": tier,
   164→        "confidence": confidence,
   165→        "summary": summary,
   166→        "detail": detail or {},
   167→        "status": "open",
   168→        "note": None,
   169→        "first_seen": now,
   170→        "last_seen": now,
   171→        "resolved_at": None,
   172→        "reopen_count": 0,
   173→    }
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   178→    return (
   179→        finding_id == pattern
   180→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   181→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   182→        or (
   183→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   184→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   185→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   186→        )
   187→    )
   188→
```

I now have a thorough understanding of the codebase. Let me provide the complete design plan.

---

## Auto-Clustering Design Plan for Desloppify

### 1. Clustering Algorithm

The algorithm must be deterministic (same findings produce same clusters) and produce human-readable task names. The key insight from studying the codebase is that finding IDs follow the pattern `detector::file::name` where `name` often encodes a subtype (e.g., `dict_keys::path/file.py::phantom_read::variable::key` or `dict_keys::path/file.py::schema_drift::key::line`).

**Two-level clustering strategy:**

**Level 1 -- Detector-wide clusters (for auto-fixable detectors):**
When a detector has `action_type == "auto_fix"` in the registry (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/registry.py`), all open findings from that detector form a single cluster because they share a single batch command (e.g., `desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run`). Examples:
- All `unused` findings -> "Clean up unused references" (action: `desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run`)
- All `logs` findings -> "Remove debug logs" (action: `desloppify fix debug-logs --dry-run`)

**Level 2 -- Detector + subtype clusters (for judgment/refactor detectors):**
For detectors with `needs_judgment == True` or `action_type` in `("refactor", "manual_fix", "reorganize")`, cluster by `(detector, subtype)` where subtype is extracted from the finding's `detail["kind"]` field (present for dict_keys, smells) or from the third `::` segment of the finding ID. When no subtype is available, cluster by `(detector, file)` to group findings that require coordinated judgment about the same file. Examples:
- `dict_keys` with `detail["kind"] == "phantom_read"` in the same file -> "Decide on phantom reads in config.py"
- `dict_keys` with `detail["kind"] == "schema_drift"` -> "Fix schema drift issues"
- `uncalled_functions` findings -> "Remove dead functions"
- `structural` findings on the same file -> "Decompose large file: service.py"

**Level 3 -- Singleton passthrough:**
Findings that end up alone after clustering remain as individual items (no cluster wrapper needed). A cluster is only created when 2+ findings group together.

**Concrete clustering key function:**

```python
def _cluster_key(finding_id: str, finding: dict) -> str:
    """Return a deterministic grouping key for a finding."""
    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    
    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix":
        # All auto-fixable findings batch together by detector
        return f"auto::{detector}"
    
    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    kind = detail.get("kind", "")
    file = finding.get("file", "")
    
    if kind:
        # Group by detector + subtype (e.g., dict_keys::phantom_read)
        return f"typed::{detector}::{kind}"
    
    if meta and meta.needs_judgment:
        # Judgment detectors group by file (coordinated refactor)
        return f"file::{detector}::{file}"
    
    # Fallback: group by detector alone
    return f"detector::{detector}"
```

**Cluster naming function** that generates human-readable names from keys:

```python
def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str, members: list[dict]) -> str:
    """Generate a slug name for the auto-cluster."""
    parts = key.split("::")
    prefix = parts[0]
    
    if prefix == "auto":
        detector = parts[1]
        return f"auto-{detector}"
    elif prefix == "typed":
        detector, kind = parts[1], parts[2]
        return f"{detector}-{kind}"
    elif prefix == "file":
        detector, file = parts[1], parts[2]
        # Use just the filename, not full path
        basename = file.rsplit("/", 1)[-1].rsplit(".", 1)[0]
        return f"{detector}-{basename}"
    else:
        return f"{parts[1]}"
```

**Cluster description** is generated from the `DetectorMeta.guidance` field plus a count.

### 2. Where Auto-Clustering Runs

Auto-clustering runs during **reconciliation after scan**, in the same block where `reconcile_plan_after_scan` and `sync_stale_dimensions` already run (lines 417-464 of `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py`).

The flow:
1. Scan completes and state is saved (line 411-415)
2. Load plan (line 427)
3. Run `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` -- handles disappeared findings (line 437)
4. Run `sync_stale_dimensions()` -- handles subjective dimension sync (line 447)
5. **NEW: Run `auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)`** -- generates/updates auto-clusters
6. Save plan if dirty (line 461-462)

This placement ensures auto-clusters reflect the latest scan results and are reconciled with any findings that disappeared.

### 3. Auto-Cluster vs Manual Cluster Distinction

**Schema change to `Cluster` TypedDict** in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`:

```python
class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    name: Required[str]
    description: str | None
    finding_ids: list[str]
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    auto: bool           # NEW: True for auto-generated clusters
    cluster_key: str     # NEW: the deterministic grouping key
    action: str | None   # NEW: primary resolution command for the cluster
    tier: int | None     # NEW: effective tier (min tier of members)
```

**Naming convention:** Auto-cluster names are prefixed with `auto/` to distinguish them from manual clusters. Examples: `auto/unused`, `auto/dict_keys-phantom_read`, `auto/structural-service`. This prefix prevents name collisions with manual clusters.

**Protection rules:**
- Auto-clusters can be deleted/modified by the user (they become "adopted" manual clusters if renamed or if items are manually added/removed).
- On rescan, auto-clusters are regenerated from scratch. Any cluster with `auto: True` that the user has NOT modified (no manual `add_to_cluster` or `remove_from_cluster` calls since last auto-generation) gets replaced. Modified auto-clusters are preserved.

To track modification, add a field:

```python
    user_modified: bool  # NEW: set True when user manually edits an auto-cluster
```

### 4. How `next` Renders a Cluster vs a Single Item

Currently `next` shows one item. With auto-clustering, when the top item belongs to a cluster, `next` should show the **cluster as a task** rather than one finding.

**Changes to `render_terminal_items()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`:**

Add a new function `_render_cluster_item()`:

```python
def _render_cluster_item(
    cluster_name: str,
    cluster: dict,
    members: list[dict],  # the finding items in this cluster
    dim_scores: dict,
    findings_scoped: dict,
    explain: bool,
    potentials: dict | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Render a cluster as a single task card."""
    # Effective tier = min tier across members
    tier = min(int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members)
    total = len(members)
    desc = cluster.get("description", "")
    action = cluster.get("action", "")
    
    print(colorize(f"  Task: {cluster_name} ({total} findings)", "bold"))
    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier})", "bold"))
    print(colorize("  " + "-" * 60, "dim"))
    if desc:
        print(f"  {colorize(desc, 'yellow')}")
    
    # Show top 3 representative findings
    print(colorize(f"\n  Includes ({total} total):", "dim"))
    for m in members[:3]:
        print(f"    - {m.get('summary', '')}")
        print(colorize(f"      {m.get('file', '')} (ID: {m.get('id', '')})", "dim"))
    if total > 3:
        print(colorize(f"    ... and {total - 3} more", "dim"))
    
    # Primary action
    if action:
        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {action}", "cyan"))
    
    # Resolution hint
    print(colorize(f"\n  Resolve all: desloppify resolve fixed \"{cluster_name}\" --note \"<what>\" --attest \"...\"", "dim"))
    print(colorize(f"  Focus on this: desloppify plan focus {cluster_name}", "dim"))
    print(colorize(f"  See all items: desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 999", "dim"))
```

**The key change in `render_terminal_items()`:** Before rendering, check if the top item belongs to a cluster. If so, gather all cluster members and render the cluster card instead of individual items.

**Changes to `build_work_queue()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`:**

Add a new step after `_apply_plan_order()` that collapses clustered items. When `count=1` (the default for `next`), if the top item belongs to a cluster, return all cluster members as the items list but mark them with a `_cluster_view` flag so the renderer knows to use the cluster rendering path.

Actually, a cleaner approach: add a `cluster_mode` option to `QueueBuildOptions`:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildOptions:
    # ... existing fields ...
    collapse_clusters: bool = True  # NEW: when True, clusters are collapsed into single entries
```

When `collapse_clusters` is True, after sorting and plan-ordering, the queue builder groups items by cluster membership and represents each cluster as a single meta-item. The meta-item has:
- `kind: "cluster"` (new kind)
- `id: cluster_name`
- `effective_tier: min(member tiers)`
- `members: [list of member items]`
- `summary: cluster description`
- `primary_command: cluster action`

This lets the sort still work by tier (cluster's min tier) and the renderer can switch on `kind == "cluster"`.

### 5. Resolution Flow for Clusters

**Batch resolution** already works via `resolve_findings(state, pattern, status)` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py`. The `_matches_pattern()` function in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py` matches by detector name (e.g., `resolve_findings(state, "unused", "fixed")` resolves all unused findings).

**New: cluster-name resolution.** Extend `_matches_pattern` or add a wrapper in the resolve command that:
1. Checks if the pattern matches a cluster name (e.g., `auto/unused`)
2. If so, resolves all `finding_ids` in that cluster

This requires a small change to the resolve command (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve_cmd.py` or equivalent) to load the plan and check cluster names before falling back to `match_findings()`.

**Partial resolution:** When a user resolves individual findings within a cluster, those findings are marked resolved in state. On next scan + reconcile, the auto-cluster is regenerated with only the remaining open findings. If the cluster shrinks to 0 or 1 members, it is automatically deleted.

### 6. De-clustering Individual Items

A user can remove an item from an auto-cluster via the existing `plan cluster remove <cluster_name> <finding_id>` command. This:
1. Calls `remove_from_cluster()` in operations.py (already implemented)
2. Sets `user_modified = True` on the cluster
3. The removed finding appears as an individual item in the queue

No new code is needed for the operation itself, just the `user_modified` tracking.

### 7. Rescan / Reconciliation Behavior

**On each scan, the auto-clustering reconciliation works as follows:**

```python
def auto_cluster_findings(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> AutoClusterResult:
    """Generate or refresh auto-clusters from current findings."""
    
    # 1. Collect all open, non-suppressed findings
    findings = {
        fid: f for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
        if f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
    }
    
    # 2. Group by cluster key
    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for fid, f in findings.items():
        key = _cluster_key(fid, f)
        groups.setdefault(key, []).append(fid)
    
    # 3. Filter out singletons
    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= 2}
    
    # 4. For each group, create or update the auto-cluster
    existing_auto = {
        name: c for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
        if c.get("auto")
    }
    
    # 5. Determine which existing auto-clusters to preserve vs regenerate
    new_cluster_names = set()
    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
        cluster_name = _auto_cluster_name(key)
        new_cluster_names.add(cluster_name)
        
        if cluster_name in existing_auto:
            old = existing_auto[cluster_name]
            if old.get("user_modified"):
                # User has customized this cluster -- don't touch it,
                # but DO add new findings that match the [REDACTED](plan, cluster_name, member_ids)
            else:
                # Replace wholesale
                _replace_auto_cluster(plan, cluster_name, member_ids, key)
        else:
            # Create new
            _create_auto_cluster(plan, cluster_name, member_ids, key)
    
    # 6. Remove stale auto-clusters (no longer have 2+ members)
    for name, c in list(existing_auto.items()):
        if name not in new_cluster_names and not c.get("user_modified"):
            delete_cluster(plan, name)
```

**Key behaviors:**
- New findings that match an existing auto-cluster key are added automatically.
- Resolved findings are removed from clusters during `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` which already calls `_supersede_id()` which removes from cluster finding_ids (line 91-94 of `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py`).
- If an auto-cluster drops to 0-1 members and is not `user_modified`, it is deleted.
- User-modified auto-clusters are preserved but augmented with new matching findings.

### 8. Auto-Cluster Interaction with Tier System

Each auto-cluster gets an `effective_tier` equal to the **minimum tier** of its members. This means:
- A cluster containing T1 findings outranks one with only T2 findings
- Within a tier, clusters sort by total member count (descending) then cluster name
- The tier navigator in `next` shows cluster counts per tier instead of individual finding counts

**Sort key for cluster meta-items:**

```python
def cluster_sort_key(cluster_item: dict) -> tuple:
    return (
        int(cluster_item.get("effective_tier", 3)),
        0,  # clusters sort before unclustered items at same tier
        -len(cluster_item.get("members", [])),  # larger clusters first
        cluster_item.get("id", ""),
    )
```

### 10. Edge Cases

**Edge case 1: Finding belongs to both a manual cluster and would be auto-clustered.**
Resolution: If a finding is already in any manual cluster (a cluster where `auto` is falsy), skip it during auto-clustering. Manual clusters take precedence.

**Edge case 2: Auto-cluster name collision with manual cluster.**
Resolution: The `auto/` prefix prevents this. Manual clusters cannot start with `auto/` (enforce in `create_cluster()`).

**Edge case 3: User focuses on an auto-cluster, then rescan regenerates it.**
Resolution: Focus (`active_cluster`) is preserved by name. Since auto-cluster names are deterministic from the grouping key, the same group of findings produces the same cluster name. Focus survives.

**Edge case 4: All findings in a cluster are resolved between scans.**
Resolution: `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` removes dead finding IDs from clusters. `auto_cluster_findings()` then sees 0 members and deletes the cluster. If the cluster was focused, focus is cleared.

**Edge case 5: A rescan splits a cluster (e.g., findings move to different files).**
Resolution: Since clustering is regenerated from scratch on each scan, the new grouping naturally creates the right clusters. Old auto-clusters that no longer match are deleted (unless `user_modified`).

**Edge case 6: `plan show` / `plan list` need to display auto-clusters.**
Resolution: The existing `_cmd_cluster_list()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` already iterates `plan["clusters"]` and displays them. Auto-clusters will appear with an `[auto]` tag.

**Edge case 7: Performance with 819 findings.**
Resolution: The clustering algorithm is O(n) -- single pass over findings to compute keys, then group. No pairwise comparisons. This is negligible compared to scan time.
